<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2054</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 930</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report alleges that IDF forces killed Gaza aid workers at point-blank range during a 2025 massacre. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份报告指控在2025年的一场屠杀中，以色列军队对加沙援助人员实施近距离射杀。这一说法再度激化关于冲突区平民保护、问责与战争报道证据收集的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47193478'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>How to delete your OpenAI account is explained in the help center, covering steps to remove data and close access. Deleting an account is a permanent action with potential data-retention caveats.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 帮助中心提供了删除账户的步骤，包含如何移除数据与关闭访问权限。请注意，删除账户是永久性操作，数据的保留可能依照平台政策执行。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47122715' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47122715' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/age-verification' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Age Verification Trap: Verifying age undermines everyone&#x27;s data protection</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年龄验证陷阱：核验年龄削弱每个人的数据保护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47122715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1652</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47122715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47122715'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mandatory age checks often force users to reveal IDs or pass through centralized validators, increasing data exposure. This undermines established data-protection guarantees and creates new attack surfaces for breaches and misuse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>强制年龄核验往往要求用户提供身份信息或通过集中验证服务进行，显著增加数据暴露风险，削弱既有的数据保护保障，并带来新的被滥用与被攻破的风险。文章强调隐私设计的重要性，呼吁采用更具隐私保护的替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=64968905&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C11%2C0%2C239' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=64968905&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C11%2C0%2C239' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47113728'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Timeframe is a family e-paper dashboard that runs on a low-power display for glanceable updates. It aggregates calendar, weather, and household notes, offering a less distracting alternative to smartphone widgets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Timeframe 是一个用于家庭的电子纸仪表板，利用低功耗显示实现一眼可览的更新。它整合日历、天气和家务简报等信息，提供比手机通知更少干扰的主页式信息源。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/live/c0l9r67drg7t' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trump&#x27;s global tariffs struck down by US Supreme Court</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47089213'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The US Supreme Court ruled against the broad tariffs, curbing presidential authority over sweeping trade measures. The decision could reshape how policymakers deploy tariffs and influence tech supply chains depending on future policy moves.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47189650' data-tags='Security,AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47189650' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/sama/status/2027578652477821175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI agrees with Dept. of War to deploy models in their classified network</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 同意与战争部在机密网络中部署模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1369</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47189650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 634</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47189650'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI reportedly agreed with the Department of War to deploy AI models on a classified government network, signaling deeper government use of advanced AI in secure environments. This raises questions about data handling, security, and governance for defense deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，OpenAI 同意与战争部在机密政府网络中部署 AI 模型，显示政府在安全环境中对先进 AI 的更深度使用。此举引发对数据处理、安全与治理在防务部署中的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1904933748015255552/k43GMz63_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1904933748015255552/k43GMz63_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47186677' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/secwar/status/2027507717469049070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I am directing the Department of War to designate Anthropic a supply-chain risk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我指示战争部将 Anthropic 列为供应链风险</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47186677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1338</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47186677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1062</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47186677'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Department of War reportedly intends to designate Anthropic as a supply-chain risk, which could curb its access to sensitive government projects and influence how agencies screen AI vendors. The move underscores growing government scrutiny of AI suppliers and the need for robust security practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称战争部计划将 Anthropic 列为供应链风险，可能限制其参与敏感政府项目并影响机构对 AI 供应商的评估。这体现政府对 AI 供应商日益严格的审查与对安全实践的强调。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

Expect modest gains in factuality and guardrails aimed at reducing harmful outputs, continuing the trend of iterative model polishing rather than a dramatic capability leap.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Sonnet 4.6，对话式 AI 进行了小幅改进，重点在可靠性和安全性上。更新提升了事实性和防护措施，旨在减少有害输出，延续逐步打磨模型的路线，而非一次性大跃进。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47188697'>

The exchange highlights tensions between defense policymakers and AI firms over deployment in national security contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 对战争部部长彼得·赫格斯特的评论作出声明，澄清在 AI 安全与政府合作方面的立场。此事揭示了国防决策者与 AI 公司在国家安全领域部署上的分歧。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/02/28/middleeast/israel-attack-iran-intl-hnk' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The United States and Israel have launched a major attack on Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美國與以色列對伊朗發動大規模攻擊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47191232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47191232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2457</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47191232'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Policy and geopolitics collide with tech in a dramatic flare as the U.S. and Israel reportedly launched a major strike on Iran. The move could escalate regional tensions and ripple through global energy prices, sanctions regimes, and international collaboration that tech companies depend on. For engineers, this highlights how geopolitical events can affect infrastructure resilience and cross-border digital cooperation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据传美國與以色列對伊朗發動重大打擊，這一行動可能升高區域緊張，並波及全球能源價格、制裁機制與依賴國際合作的科技供應鏈。地緣政治動盪或引發網路安全對抗與政策變化，影響雲端、AI 等領域的韌性與成本。科技讀者應關注此類事件對基礎設施與跨境協作的潛在影響。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/ap26059256811672.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' data-full='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/ap26059256811672.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47045612' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47045612' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.tomaszdunia.pl/grapheneos-eng/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS – Break Free from Google and Apple</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS —— 摆脱谷歌和苹果的束缚</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47045612' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47045612' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 826</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47045612'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS is an open-source Android-based operating system focused on privacy and security, aiming to provide a Google- and Apple-free mobile experience. By hardening the underlying OS and minimizing data collection, it appeals to users wary of mainstream ecosystems’ surveillance and control. This project highlights growing demand for more transparent, user-controlled smartphone platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 是一个基于安卓的开源操作系统，专注于隐私和安全，致力于提供无谷歌、无苹果的手机体验。通过强化系统安全和减少数据收集，它吸引了对主流生态系统监控和控制心存疑虑的用户。该项目反映了对更透明、用户可控智能手机平台的日益需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.tomaszdunia.pl/images/grapheneos.png' data-full='https://blog.tomaszdunia.pl/images/grapheneos.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community-driven effort credited Hacker News readers with mobilizing resources that led to saving 33,000 lives, showing how online coordination can scale for public-health or crisis responses. The case underlines the potential impact of technical communities when they contribute expertise, funding, or logistics to urgent real-world problems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次社区主导的行动将挽救 3.3 万条生命的成就归功于 Hacker News 读者，体现了网络协调在公共卫生或危机响应中的规模化能力。这个例子强调技术社区在紧急现实问题上贡献专业知识、资金或后勤时能产生的显著影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://karpathy.github.io/2026/02/12/microgpt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microgpt</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MicroGPT（微型GPT）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1058</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47202708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47202708'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Karpathy unveils MicroGPT, a compact, self-contained GPT-inspired framework designed to run with minimal dependencies. It targets experimentation on modest hardware and on-device reasoning, enabling offline AI tooling and edge applications. This approach lowers the barrier to LM research beyond cloud deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Karpathy 介紹了 MicroGPT——一個極簡、可在低依賴環境下運行的 GPT 類框架，適合在中等硬件上試驗與本地推理。它促進離線 AI 工具與邊緣應用的發展，讓 LM 研究不再僅限於雲端。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47190997' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47190997' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://help.openai.com/en/articles/7232927-how-do-i-cancel-my-chatgpt-subscription' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How do I cancel my ChatGPT subscription?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何取消我的 ChatGPT 订阅？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47190997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1033</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47190997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47190997'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Steps to cancel ChatGPT subscriptions are documented in help; you can navigate to account settings, manage subscription, and confirm cancellation. Your plan will stop auto-renewal and access after the current cycle.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帮助文档列出取消 ChatGPT 订阅的具体步骤：进入账户设置、管理订阅并确认取消。取消后将停止自动续订，当前周期结束后服务将终止。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47057829' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47057829' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nvie.com/posts/15-years-later/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>15 years later, Microsoft morged my diagram</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>15 年后，微软盗用了我的图示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47057829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 992</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47057829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 374</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47057829'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author recounts discovering that a diagram they created 15 years ago was appropriated by Microsoft without proper attribution, raising questions about corporate reuse of independent work. The story highlights how intellectual contributions from individuals can be absorbed into large organizations&#x27; materials, and why maintaining provenance and licensing matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者讲述发现自己 15 年前绘制的图示被微软在未适当归属的情况下使用，引发对企业挪用独立作品的质疑。该事件凸显个人知识成果容易被大组织吸收，并提醒人们关注来源记录和许可问题的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nvie.com/img/nvie-small@2x.jpg' data-full='https://nvie.com/img/nvie-small@2x.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://boristane.com/blog/how-i-use-claude-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How I use Claude Code: Separation of planning and execution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我在使用 Claude Code 时如何将规划与执行分离</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47106686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 918</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47106686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 562</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47106686'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post shares a practical workflow for Claude Code that separates planning from execution steps when building code. By drafting a plan first and then executing it in stages, the author reduces misfires and makes debugging easier, offering tips like staged prompts and independent tests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文分享了一种将 Claude Code 的规划与执行分离的实际工作流。先拟定计划再分阶段执行，有助于减少失误并便于调试，文中还给出分步提示和独立测试等实用要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47038546' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47038546' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/innovation/this-14-year-old-is-using-origami-to-design-emergency-shelters-that-are-sturdy-cost-efficient-and-easy-to-deploy-180988179/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>14-year-old Miles Wu folded origami pattern that holds 10k times its own weight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>14岁少年设计摺纸结构，能承载自身体重1万倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 906</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47038546'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>At 14, Miles Wu engineered an origami-based pattern capable of supporting 10,000 times its own weight, applied in designing practical emergency shelters. His innovation shows how ancient art principles can intersect with modern engineering for lightweight, durable, and affordable structures. This approach could revolutionize rapid-deployment housing in disaster relief.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>14岁的Miles Wu设计了一种基于摺纸的结构样式，能承载自身体重的1万倍，用于紧急避难所。这个创新展示了古老艺术与现代工程的结合，实现轻便、耐用且经济的建筑。此方法有望革新灾难救援中的快速部署住房。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/wUPPgpDHXxQh_Fz5JBjAfVAkLV4=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1000x667:1001x668)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/f6/6e/f66edaff-eb72-4b3e-8e69-77bf70f7aa4d/20251025_public-day_wu_miles_0135_lf.jpg' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/wUPPgpDHXxQh_Fz5JBjAfVAkLV4=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(1000x667:1001x668)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/f6/6e/f66edaff-eb72-4b3e-8e69-77bf70f7aa4d/20251025_public-day_wu_miles_0135_lf.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47172119' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/jack/status/2027129697092731343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Layoffs at Block</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Block 宣布裁员</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47172119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 888</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47172119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1044</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47172119'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Block confirms layoffs as part of a restructuring amid a cooling tech and fintech environment. The cuts signal a continued belt-tightening in the sector and may shift focus toward core platforms and profitability. Employee and roadmap implications are likely as the company reallocates resources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Block 确认裁员，作为结构调整的一部分，反映出科技与金融科技领域的收缩态势。裁员可能会推动公司将资源集中在核心平台和盈利能力上，并影响员工和产品路线图。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1661201415899951105/azNjKOSH_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1661201415899951105/azNjKOSH_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://garymarcus.substack.com/p/the-whole-thing-was-scam' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The whole thing was a scam</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>整個事情都是詐騙</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 867</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197505' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47197505'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gary Marcus argues that a celebrated AI initiative or narrative was built on hype and misrepresentations. The piece calls out inflated claims, flawed methods, and incentives that rewarded hype over real engineering progress. It serves as a cautionary reminder to scrutinize AI marketing versus tangible engineering advances.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gary Marcus 指出某個知名 AI 計畫或敘事建立在炒作與誤導之上，揭露夸大聲稱與方法論缺陷，以及把炒作利益置於真正工程進展之上的動機。文章提醒讀者要區辨 AI 行銷與實際工程成就，避免被包裝過的繁榮所誤導。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://annas-archive.li/blog/llms-txt.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you’re an LLM, please read this</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47058219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 861</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47058219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 382</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47058219'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A plea-style piece directed at large language models lays out expectations for honesty, safety, and better alignment with human norms, framed as straightforward instructions LLMs should follow. It underscores community frustration with repetitive, evasive, or unsafe model behaviors and calls for clearer incentives and engineering to fix them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇面向大型语言模型的文章列出了对诚实、安全以及更好符合人类规范的具体期待，像是给模型的行为指南。文中表达了社区对模型重复、回避或不安全行为的失望，并呼吁通过更清晰的激励和工程手段加以改进。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47088037'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GGML, a lightweight library for local AI inference, joins Hugging Face to push standardization and collaboration for offline AI. The move signals a push toward privacy-preserving on-device AI and broader ecosystem cooperation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GGML 作为本地 AI 推理的轻量库加入 Hugging Face，旨在推动离线 AI 的标准化与协作。此举体现了对隐私保护的本地化 AI 与更广泛生态协作的推进。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Danish government agency plans to ditch Microsoft software as part of digital independence, outlining a transition path to open-source or alternative tools.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47066552' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47066552' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pudding.cool/2026/02/womens-sizing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sizing chaos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尺码混乱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47066552' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 804</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47066552' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 419</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47066552'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive investigation into women&#x27;s clothing sizing shows vast inconsistencies across brands and regions, making online shopping and size selection unreliable. The analysis visualizes measurement distributions and highlights the need for standardization or better metadata so consumers and designers can find consistent fits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项交互式调查揭示了女性服装尺码在品牌和地区间的大量不一致，导致线上购物和尺码选择变得不可靠。文章通过可视化测量分布，强调需要标准化或更好元数据，帮助消费者和设计师找到一致的合身方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pudding.cool/2026/02/womens-sizing/assets/og2.jpg' data-full='https://pudding.cool/2026/02/womens-sizing/assets/og2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47086181' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47086181' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://taalas.com/the-path-to-ubiquitous-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The path to ubiquitous AI (17k tokens/sec)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>走向普及化 AI 的路径（每秒 17k tokens）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47086181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 798</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47086181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 431</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47086181'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores a roadmap to ubiquitous AI by tackling extremely high inference throughput, citing 17k tokens per second as a benchmark. The piece surveys optimization strategies across hardware and software and discusses implications for real-time AI at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦极高推理吞吐量的路线图，以每秒 17k token 作为基准，讨论硬件与软件的优化策略，以及对大规模实时 AI 的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://taalas.com/h-content/themes/h-theme-child/media/blank-featured.png' data-full='https://taalas.com/h-content/themes/h-theme-child/media/blank-featured.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47181208' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47181208' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcgamer.com/software/operating-systems/a-new-california-law-says-all-operating-systems-including-linux-need-to-have-some-form-of-age-verification-at-account-setup/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new California law says all operating systems need to have age verification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州新法规定所有操作系统在设置账户时需进行年龄验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 794</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 685</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47181208'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California passes a law requiring age verification at OS setup for all devices, including Linux. The move aims to curb underage access but could complicate privacy and OSS distribution, forcing platforms to implement new authentication flows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州通过新法，要求所有操作系统在账户设置时进行年龄验证，覆盖包括 Linux 在内的设备。此举旨在限制未成年人使用，同时可能带来隐私与开源软件分发的合规挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/Z9XbAPKf32a28g9TdTi8dG-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/Z9XbAPKf32a28g9TdTi8dG-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47115805' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47115805' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/account-restricted-without-warning-google-ai-ultra-oauth-via-openclaw/122778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google restricting Google AI Pro/Ultra subscribers for using OpenClaw</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌限制 Google AI Pro/Ultra 用户使用 OpenClaw</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47115805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 793</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47115805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 685</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47115805'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is restricting Google AI Pro/Ultra subscribers from using OpenClaw, a tool some users employ to automate tasks. The move highlights how platform policies can disrupt automation workflows for power users of AI services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，谷歌限制 Google AI Pro/Ultra 用户通过 OpenClaw 等工具进行自动化操作。此举凸显平台政策对高阶用户自动化工作流的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d3qe71uytubmmx.cloudfront.net/original/2X/8/8bfe64fc593eb7baba8df149ebba4457c16ca1f9.png' data-full='https://d3qe71uytubmmx.cloudfront.net/original/2X/8/8bfe64fc593eb7baba8df149ebba4457c16ca1f9.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47055979' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47055979' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/02/17/ai-productivity-paradox-ceo-study-robert-solow-information-technology-age/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI adoption and Solow&#x27;s productivity paradox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能采纳与索洛的生产率悖论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47055979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 779</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47055979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 725</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47055979'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece revisits Solow&#x27;s productivity paradox in the context of accelerating AI adoption, questioning why large AI investments haven&#x27;t translated into obvious productivity gains yet. It explores measurement problems, lagging complementary investments, and organizational frictions that could explain the gap between AI hype and macroeconomic productivity metrics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章在加速采用 AI 的背景下重新审视索洛的生产率悖论，探讨为何大量 AI 投资尚未明显反映在生产率上。文中分析了衡量困难、配套投资滞后和组织摩擦等因素，试图解释 AI 热潮与宏观生产率数据之间的落差。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-521488522-e1771352623863.jpg?resize=1200,600' data-full='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-521488522-e1771352623863.jpg?resize=1200,600' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47151233' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47151233' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.0xsid.com/blog/online-tld-is-pain' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Never buy a .online domain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要买 .online 域名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47151233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 770</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47151233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 485</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47151233'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues against purchasing a .online domain due to branding, trust, and SEO drawbacks, plus the risk of confusion with similar domains. It urges choosing a more reputable TLD for lasting credibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文劝说读者不要购买 .online 域名，理由包括品牌信任、搜索引擎优化和长期维护成本等，以及与同类域名的混淆风险。建议选择更具信誉的顶级域名以建立长期信任。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wisp.ssiddharth.com/img/wisp-namecheap.png' data-full='https://wisp.ssiddharth.com/img/wisp-namecheap.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47061614' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47061614' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.deadlanguagesociety.com/p/how-far-back-in-time-understand-english' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How far back in time can you understand English?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你能理解多早期的英语？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47061614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 723</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47061614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 361</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47061614'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Old English, Middle English, and Early Modern English each present distinct vocabulary, grammar, and spelling gaps from modern usage. The piece surveys how far a typical reader can understand these stages and what tools help bridge the distance, such as glossaries and annotated editions. It notes that comprehension improves with exposure, but some periods remain opaque without study.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>古英语、中古英语和早期现代英语在词汇、语法和拼写上与现代英语存在显著差异。本文探讨普通读者在多大程度上能理解这些阶段，并介绍通过词汇表、注释版等工具来缩小差距的做法。理解能力会随着接触而提升，但若不系统学习，某些时期仍难以读懂。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!94Uq!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5dddb379-4714-4730-bb8d-538c26e2d623_1600x1066.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!94Uq!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5dddb379-4714-4730-bb8d-538c26e2d623_1600x1066.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47056314' data-tags='Culture,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47056314' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sceneandheardnu.com/content/halt-and-catch-fire' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Halt and Catch Fire: TV’s best drama you’ve probably never heard of (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《黑客军团》：你可能没听过的最佳剧集（2021）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47056314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 717</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47056314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 379</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47056314'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A recommendation arguing that the TV drama &#x27;Halt and Catch Fire&#x27; is an underrated gem, praised for its character-driven storytelling about the 1980s–90s tech boom. The show is noted for realistic portrayals of engineering culture, startup dynamics, and evolving personal relationships over multiple seasons.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇推荐称《Halt and Catch Fire》是一部被低估的好剧，以人物驱动的叙事讲述 80 至 90 年代的科技浪潮。该剧在工程文化、创业动态以及多年人物关系演变方面被认为相当真实。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/59066a076b8f5b6083962bff/t/606b3771a0a113500b35b2cb/1617639281578/download+%287%29.jfif?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/59066a076b8f5b6083962bff/t/606b3771a0a113500b35b2cb/1617639281578/download+%287%29.jfif?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47085483' data-tags='Startups,Privacy,DevOps,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47085483' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.coinerella.com/made-in-eu-it-was-harder-than-i-thought/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I tried building my startup entirely on European infrastructure</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我尝试把初创公司完全建立在欧洲基础设施上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47085483' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 712</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47085483' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 364</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47085483'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A founder documents the experience of relying exclusively on European cloud and edge services, highlighting regulatory frictions, data residency quirks, and supplier diversity trade-offs. The takeaway: sovereignty is compelling but comes with pain points for speed and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>创始人记录了完全依赖欧洲云与边缘基础设施的经历，强调监管摩擦、数据驻留与供应商多样性取舍等问题。结论是：自主性很有吸引力，但在速度与工具链上也带来挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1608817576203-3c27ed168bd2?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDF8fGV1cm9wZXxlbnwwfHx8fDE3NzE0MDQ5Mjl8MA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1608817576203-3c27ed168bd2?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDF8fGV1cm9wZXxlbnwwfHx8fDE3NzE0MDQ5Mjl8MA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47152085' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47152085' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.marginalia.nu/weird-ai-crap/hn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New accounts on HN more likely to use em-dashes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HN 新账户更常用长破折号</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47152085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47152085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 596</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47152085'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at a trend where new Hacker News accounts frequently feature em-dashes in usernames or post formatting, pointing to evolving user signaling. The pattern matters for moderation and signal quality in a high-traffic tech community.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观察到新注册的 HN 用户名常用长破折号，成为信号表达的一种风格。该趋势影响社区信号质量与版规执行，尤其在高流量环境中。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47127081' data-tags='Privacy,Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47127081' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/02/23/americans-are-destroying-flock-surveillance-cameras/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Americans are destroying Flock surveillance cameras</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国人正在破坏 Flock 的监控摄像头</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47127081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 700</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47127081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 483</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47127081'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rising privacy activism leads individuals to vandalize or remove license-plate cameras from a private vendor, highlighting tensions between public safety tech and civil liberties. The incident underscores how consumer-privacy backlash can impact surveillance infrastructure and vendor deployments. It raises questions about the resilience and governance of city-scale surveillance programs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着隐私行动主义的兴起，个人对监控设备的破坏和撤下行为增多，凸显公共安全技术与公民自由之间的张力。事件暴露出执法与商业监控基础设施在治理、韧性与隐私保护上的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.marginalia.nu/log/a_132_ai_bores/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI makes you boring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 让你变得无聊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47076966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 687</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47076966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 368</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47076966'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A brisk critique of AI-assisted creativity, arguing that reliance on AI prompts can erode original thinking and make output feel generic. It offers practical ways to preserve authenticity and maintain edge as AI tools mature.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AI 辅助创作的尖锐批评，认为过度依赖 AI 提示会削弱原创性，让输出变得平庸。给出在 AI 工具日益成熟时保持真实感与竞争力的可行做法。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thebignewsletter.com/p/amazon-busted-for-widespread-price' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazon accused of widespread scheme to inflate prices across the economy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊被指控在全经济范围内普遍抬高价格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47145907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 674</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47145907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47145907'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Regulatory findings allege widespread pricing schemes at Amazon that inflated consumer prices across categories. The report enters antitrust and competition debates and could affect how marketplaces price and disclose promotions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称亚马逊存在大规模定价行为，抬高了多个品类的消费者价格，进入反垄断与竞争领域的议题，可能影响平台的定价与促销披露。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47157224' data-tags='Culture,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47157224' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/jimi-hendrix-systems-engineer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jimi Hendrix was a systems engineer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>吉米·亨德里克斯其实是系统工程师</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47157224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 660</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47157224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 236</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47157224'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jimi Hendrix’s work blended electronics and systemic thinking, earning him a reputation as a systems engineer in practice. The piece reframes his guitar innovations as early examples of complex-systems thinking in music tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>吉米·亨德里克斯的创作把电子学与系统思考融为一体，实践中堪称系统工程师。文章将他的吉他创新解读为音乐科技中复杂系统思维的早期案例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.png?id=64983431&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C344%2C0%2C344' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.png?id=64983431&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C344%2C0%2C344' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47163885' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47163885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: YC companies scrape GitHub activity, send spam emails to users</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tell HN: YC 公司抓取 GitHub 活动并向用户发送垃圾邮件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47163885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 649</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47163885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47163885'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report alleges YC-backed startups harvest GitHub activity data and email users at scale, raising privacy and consent concerns in developer ecosystems. The post spotlights tensions between growth tactics and user privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称部分 YC 支持的初创公司会规模化抓取 GitHub 活动数据并向用户发送邮件，引发开发者生态系的隐私与同意问题。这一讨论凸显增长手段与隐私之间的张力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47110515' data-tags='Web,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47110515' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://susam.net/attention-media-vs-social-networks.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Attention Media ≠ Social Networks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>注意力媒体≠社交网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 645</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47110515'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Media attention and social networks are not the same thing; attention economics often treats content discovery and platform design differently. The piece argues for designing systems that measure and monetize attention without conflating it with the social graph.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>媒体关注度与社交网络并非同一概念；注意力经济往往把内容发现与平台设计区分对待。该文主张在衡量和变现注意力时，避免与社交关系网混为一谈。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vmfunc.re/blog/persona/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI, the US government and Persona built an identity surveillance machine</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47140632'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative look at how OpenAI, the US government, and Persona reportedly collaborated to build a broad identity surveillance pipeline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章揭示 OpenAI、美国政府与 Persona 可能共同打造的身份监控体系，讨论在跨服务集中身份数据所带来的隐私与公民自由风险，以及相关技术挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://time.com/7380854/exclusive-anthropic-drops-flagship-safety-pledge/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic Drops Flagship Safety Pledge</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 放弃旗舰安全承诺</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47145963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 636</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47145963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47145963'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic reportedly drops its flagship safety pledge, raising questions about commitments to safety, regulatory expectations, and how customers evaluate risk in AI products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 传出取消旗舰安全承诺的消息，引发对其安全承诺、监管期望及用户风险评估方式的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://api.time.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/Anthropic.jpg?quality=85&amp;w=1200&amp;h=628&amp;crop=1' data-full='https://api.time.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/Anthropic.jpg?quality=85&amp;w=1200&amp;h=628&amp;crop=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47069299' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47069299' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.claude.com/docs/en/legal-and-compliance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic officially bans using subscription auth for third party use</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 正式禁止将订阅认证用于第三方使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47069299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 634</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47069299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 762</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47069299'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic updated its policy to ban using individual subscription authentication credentials for third-party integrations, tightening access controls and preventing workaround uses. This change forces developers to adopt official API or enterprise flows for multi-user or embedded access, affecting small-scale integrations that relied on personal accounts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 更新政策，禁止将个人订阅认证用于第三方集成，收紧了访问控制并阻止规避性用法。此变更要求开发者使用官方 API 或企业授权来实现多用户或嵌入式访问，影响此前依赖个人账户的小规模集成。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DResources%26appearance%3Dsystem%26title%3DLegal%2Band%2Bcompliance%26description%3DLegal%2Bagreements%252C%2Bcompliance%2Bcertifications%252C%2Band%2Bsecurity%2Binformation%2Bfor%2BClaude%2BCode.%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fo69F7a6qoW9vboof%252Flogo%252Flight.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Do69F7a6qoW9vboof%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D536eade682636e84231afce2577f9509%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fo69F7a6qoW9vboof%252Flogo%252Fdark.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Do69F7a6qoW9vboof%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D0766b3221061e80143e9f300733e640b%26primaryColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26lightColor%3D%2523D4A27F%26darkColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26backgroundLight%3D%2523FDFDF7%26backgroundDark%3D%252309090B&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' data-full='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DResources%26appearance%3Dsystem%26title%3DLegal%2Band%2Bcompliance%26description%3DLegal%2Bagreements%252C%2Bcompliance%2Bcertifications%252C%2Band%2Bsecurity%2Binformation%2Bfor%2BClaude%2BCode.%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fo69F7a6qoW9vboof%252Flogo%252Flight.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Do69F7a6qoW9vboof%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D536eade682636e84231afce2577f9509%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fo69F7a6qoW9vboof%252Flogo%252Fdark.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Do69F7a6qoW9vboof%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D0766b3221061e80143e9f300733e640b%26primaryColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26lightColor%3D%2523D4A27F%26darkColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26backgroundLight%3D%2523FDFDF7%26backgroundDark%3D%252309090B&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47075124' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47075124' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://micasa.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Micasa – track your house from the terminal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Micasa – 通过终端跟踪你的房子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47075124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 630</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47075124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47075124'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Micasa is a CLI tool that lets you monitor your home from the terminal, offering lightweight, scriptable observability without a full UI. It showcases how developers can ship practical, privacy-friendly home-monitoring tooling with minimal friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Micasa 是一个通过终端监控家庭的命令行工具，提供轻量、可脚本化的观测能力，无需完整界面。它展示了开发者如何在最小摩擦下交付实用且更注重隐私的家居监控工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://micasa.dev/images/demo.webp' data-full='https://micasa.dev/images/demo.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47094192' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47094192' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://words.filippo.io/dependabot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turn Dependabot off</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关闭 Dependabot</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47094192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 626</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47094192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 182</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47094192'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues for turning off Dependabot automations due to noise, risk considerations, or strategic preferences. Discusses alternatives like manual reviews or managed update policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论述因噪声、风险或策略偏好而关闭 Dependabot 自动更新的理由，并探讨手动审查或受控更新策略等替代方案。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47143152' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47143152' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/02/apple-accelerates-us-manufacturing-with-mac-mini-production/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mac mini will be made at a new facility in Houston</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mac mini 将在休斯顿新工厂生产</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47143152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 623</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47143152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 660</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47143152'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple expands domestic manufacturing with a new Mac mini production site in Houston, part of a broader push to reshore chip-and-device production. The move likely creates jobs and reduces supply-chain risk for a popular desktop.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果宣布在休斯顿设立新工厂扩产 Mac mini，这是其推动回流制造的一部分，预计带来就业并降低供应链风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/02/apple-accelerates-us-manufacturing-with-mac-mini-production/tile/Apple-US-manufacturing-investment-Houston-hero-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202602241059' data-full='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/02/apple-accelerates-us-manufacturing-with-mac-mini-production/tile/Apple-US-manufacturing-investment-Houston-hero-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202602241059' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47181391' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47181391' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/02/victory-tenth-circuit-finds-fourth-amendment-doesnt-support-broad-search-0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Court finds Fourth Amendment doesn’t support broad search of protesters’ devices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法院裁定第四修正案不支持对抗议者设备的广泛搜索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 621</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47181391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47181391'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court held that the Fourth Amendment does not broadly authorize searching protesters’ devices, marking a significant privacy protection for digital evidence. The ruling narrows police power to seize or inspect personal data at demonstrations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院裁定第四修正案不支持对抗议者设备的广泛搜索，强化了数字隐私保护。这一裁决缩小了警方在示威现场查验个人数据的权限。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/protest-2024-2.jpg' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/protest-2024-2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47200420' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/OpenAI/status/2027846016423321831' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We do not think Anthropic should be designated as a supply chain risk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我們認為 Anthropic 不應被視為供應鏈風險</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 600</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 307</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47200420'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI clarifies that Anthropic should not be treated as a special supply-chain risk, arguing for standard vendor diligence instead. This stance underscores how businesses weigh safety concerns against collaboration when sourcing AI services. It reflects the ongoing debate over risk labeling in enterprise AI sourcing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 明確表示 Anthropic 不應被視為特殊的供應鏈風險，主張以常規供應商盡職調查來評估風險。此立場顯示企業在採購 AI 服務時如何在安全與協作之間取得平衡，亦反映關於風險分類的持續爭論。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47167858' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/ai/nano-banana-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nano Banana 2: Google&#x27;s latest AI image generation model</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 598</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 571</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47167858'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nano Banana 2 introduces a more efficient image-generation model from Google, aiming to maintain high quality with lower compute. The release underscores Google’s push to scale image synthesis and has implications for developers, content pipelines, and potential on-device capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nano Banana 2 引入一款高效的 AI 图像生成模型，旨在在较低算力下保持质量。此举体现谷歌在图像合成领域的扩张策略，将影响开发者工作流、内容生产链以及设备端能力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47092006' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47092006' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/tech-policy/2026/02/wikipedia-bans-archive-today-after-site-executed-ddos-and-altered-web-captures/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wikipedia deprecates Archive.today, starts removing archive links</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>维基百科弃用 Archive.today，开始撤下存档链接</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47092006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 586</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47092006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 355</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47092006'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wikipedia moves away from Archive.today after incidents of DDoS and altered captures, affecting how citations are archived. The change pushes readers toward other archiving methods or Wikipedia&#x27;s own archives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>维基百科因 Archive.today 遭遇的 DDoS 与快照修改问题，决定弃用并逐步移除存档链接，影响引用的历史追溯性，并推动使用其他存档方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/wikipedia-1152x648-1771611351.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/wikipedia-1152x648-1771611351.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pi：一个极简终端编码框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47143754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 577</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47143754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 297</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47143754'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pi is a minimal terminal coding harness designed to experiment with small, focused coding tasks in a lightweight environment. It aims to lower setup friction for trying new languages and APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pi 是一个极简终端编码框架，便于在轻量环境中完成小型、聚焦的编码练习，降低尝试新语言和 API 的门槛。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47113618' data-tags='Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joinloops.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Loops is a federated, open-source TikTok</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Loops 是一个联邦制、开源的 TikTok 式平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 566</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 384</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47113618'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Loops is a federated, open-source alternative to TikTok, where short videos live on independent servers rather than a single centralized platform. It aims to give users more control and resilience by distributing content across the network.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Loops 是一个联邦化、开源的 TikTok 替代方案，短视频在各自的节点服务器上分发而非集中在一个平台。它旨在通过网络分发提升用户控制权与抗审查性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://joinloops.org/__og-image__/image/og.png' data-full='https://joinloops.org/__og-image__/image/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47181211' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47181211' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/02/27/openai-raises-110b-in-one-of-the-largest-private-funding-rounds-in-history/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI raises $110B on $730B pre-money valuation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 完成 1100 亿美元融资，估值约 7300 亿美元（融资前）</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47042396' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cx2gn239exlo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dark web agent spotted bedroom wall clue to rescue girl from abuse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>暗网卧底通过卧室墙壁线索救出受虐女孩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47042396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 556</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47042396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47042396'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A dark web investigator noticed a hidden clue on a bedroom wall, which played a critical role in rescuing a girl from abuse. This case exemplifies how digital vigilance and unconventional intelligence gathering can aid law enforcement in uncovering hidden crimes. It also raises awareness about the covert ways victims might try to signal for help.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名暗网调查员在卧室墙壁上发现隐藏线索，成为救援受虐女孩的关键。此案展示了数字监控和非传统情报收集如何帮助执法部门揭露隐秘犯罪。同时也提醒人们受害者可能通过隐蔽方式求助的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/5ef8/live/d681e5e0-0827-11f1-b5e2-dd58fc65f0f6.png' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/5ef8/live/d681e5e0-0827-11f1-b5e2-dd58fc65f0f6.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ewtnnews.com/vatican/pope-leo-xiv-tells-priests-to-use-their-brains-not-ai-to-write-homilies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pope tells priests to use their brains, not AI, to write homilies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>教皇让神父用脑子而非AI来写讲道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47119210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 551</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47119210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 438</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47119210'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pope Francis urges priests to rely on human judgment rather than AI to craft homilies, signaling cautious boundaries for AI in sacred duties. The stance reflects broader debates about automation&#x27;s role in culturally sensitive work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>教皇方济各呼吁神父在撰写讲道时优先运用人类判断，而非依赖 AI，标志着在宗教敏感工作中对 AI 的审慎边界。其观点映射出自动化在文化性强工作中的广泛辩论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/02/23/technology/binance-employees-iran-firings.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Binance fired employees who found $1.7B in crypto was sent to Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>币安解雇发现1.7亿美元加密资金流向伊朗的员工</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47127396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47127396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47127396'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Whistleblowers reportedly flagged a large crypto transfer to Iran, prompting Binance to terminate the employees who raised the concern. The episode spotlights compliance risk, sanctions evasion, and the potential chilling effect on internal reporting within exchanges. It also raises questions about how exchanges monitor flows and protect employees who surface potential violations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>内部举报者据称发现有价值1.7亿美元的加密资金流向伊朗，随后被币安解雇。事件凸显合规、制裁规避与对内部举报者的保护之间的难题，也引发关于交易所如何监控资金流与保护员工的讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dispatch.techoversight.org/top-report-mark-zuckerberg-lied-to-congress-we-cant-trust-his-testimony/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mark Zuckerberg Lied to Congress. We Can&#x27;t Trust His Testimony</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扎克伯格在国会撒谎，我们不能信任他的证词</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47060486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47060486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 316</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47060486'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report accuses Mark Zuckerberg of providing false testimony to Congress, arguing that his statements undermine trust in executive accountability for platform harms. The claim, if substantiated, would have implications for regulatory oversight and the credibility of corporate leaders testifying before lawmakers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份报告指控马克·扎克伯格在国会作伪证，认为其陈述削弱了对平台负责人问责的信任。若指控成立，将影响监管监督力度并损及企业高管在国会前证词的公信力。</p>
