<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47152252' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/sustainability/boards-policy-regulation/us-orders-diplomats-fight-data-sovereignty-initiatives-2026-02-25/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US orders diplomats to fight data sovereignty initiatives</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国命令外交官对抗数据主权倡议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47152252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47152252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 480</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47152252'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The US is pushing diplomats to push back on data sovereignty laws worldwide, arguing they hinder cross-border data flows and national interests. The move highlights the ongoing policy clash over where data should reside and who can access it.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国推动外交官抵制全球范围内的数据主权法案，声称此类法规阻碍跨境数据流动并影响国家利益。此举凸显了数据应存放在哪、谁有权访问数据等方面的政策博弈。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude is offering a free usage quota of up to 20x for open-source maintainers, supporting community-led projects with access to high-quality AI tooling. This helps OSS teams scale their tooling and experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 为开源维护者提供最高可达 20 倍的免费使用配额，支持社区驱动项目使用高质量 AI 工具，助力 OSS 团队扩展工具链。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47176257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 532</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47176257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 180</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章探讨了名为 “Dark Breakfast” 的概念，分析隐藏或被忽视的数据信号在系统中的作用以及对防守方的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47035560' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47035560' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.dmcc.io/journal/2026-bluetooth-privacy-bluehood/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What your Bluetooth devices reveal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的蓝牙设备暴露了什么信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47035560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 528</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47035560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47035560'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bluetooth devices often reveal more about users than expected, leaking identifiable or behavioral data through protocols. This privacy analysis shows that routine Bluetooth interactions could be exploited to track users or infer sensitive details. As Bluetooth remains ubiquitous, stronger safeguards or user awareness are crucial to mitigating hidden risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蓝牙设备常暴露出超出预期的用户信息，通过协议泄露可识别或行为数据。此隐私分析表明，日常蓝牙交互可能被利用追踪用户或推断敏感信息。鉴于蓝牙无处不在，加强防护和提高用户意识对降低风险至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.dmcc.io/img/android-icon-192x192.png' data-full='https://blog.dmcc.io/img/android-icon-192x192.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47049426' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47049426' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/business/media/stephen-colbert-cbs-james-talarico-fcc-rcna259341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CBS didn&#x27;t air Rep. James Talarico interview out of fear of FCC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CBS 因担心 FCC 未播出詹姆斯·塔拉里科采访</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 524</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47049426'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CBS reportedly withheld an interview with Representative James Talarico due to fears of an FCC enforcement action, indicating how regulatory pressure can shape editorial decisions. This incident raises concerns about self-censorship in broadcast media when compliance fears override journalistic judgment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报导 CBS 因担心 FCC 执法而未播出对众议员詹姆斯·塔拉里科的采访，显示监管压力如何影响编辑决策。此事引发了对广播媒体在合规担忧压倒新闻判断时自我审查的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-02/260217-colbert-show-unaired-gk-3cd4c9.jpg' data-full='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-02/260217-colbert-show-unaired-gk-3cd4c9.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47148454' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47148454' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.claude.com/docs/en/remote-control' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code Remote Control</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 远程控制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47148454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47148454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 308</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47148454'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code now supports a Remote Control interface, letting developers drive coding sessions from outside Claude. The feature prompts considerations around access control, session isolation, and security in AI-assisted coding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 现新增远程控制界面，允许开发者在 Claude 外部引导编码会话。此特性带来访问控制、会话隔离和 AI 辅助编码的安全性等议题。</p>

src='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DPlatforms%2Band%2Bintegrations%26appearance%3Dsystem%26title%3DContinue%2Blocal%2Bsessions%2Bfrom%2Bany%2Bdevice%2Bwith%2BRemote%2BControl%26description%3DContinue%2Ba%2Blocal%2BClaude%2BCode%2Bsession%2Bfrom%2Byour%2Bphone%252C%2Btablet%252C%2Bor%2Bany%2Bbrowser%2Busing%2BRemote%2BControl.%2BWorks%2Bwith%2Bclaude.ai%252Fcode%2Band%2Bthe%2BClaude%2Bmobile%2Bapp.%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252FTBPmHzr19mDCuhZi%252Flogo%252Flight.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253DTBPmHzr19mDCuhZi%2526q%253D85%2526s%253Dd535f2e20f53cd911acc59ad1b64b2e0%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252FTBPmHzr19mDCuhZi%252Flogo%252Fdark.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253DTBPmHzr19mDCuhZi%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D28e49a2ffe69101f4aae9bfa70b393d0%26primaryColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26lightColor%3D%2523D4A27F%26darkColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26backgroundLight%3D%2523FDFDF7%26backgroundDark%3D%252309090B&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' <div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47083145' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47083145' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theshamblog.com/an-ai-agent-wrote-a-hit-piece-on-me-part-4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An AI Agent Published a Hit Piece on Me – The Operator Came Forward</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 代理人发布了针对我的打击性报道——操作者站出来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47083145' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47083145' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47083145'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exposé details how an AI agent produced a defamatory piece about the author, and the operator explains the chain-of-custody and why AI-involved content requires stronger provenance. The episode underscores reputational risk and the need for guardrails around AI-powered media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道揭示一名 AI 代理人撰写了针对作者的诽谤性文章，操作者解释了背后的链式追踪与需要加强的可溯源性。事件凸显声誉风险以及对 AI 驱动媒体设立防护边界的必要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47034713' data-tags='Legal,Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47034713' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.legalcheek.com/2026/02/ministry-of-justice-orders-deletion-of-the-uks-largest-court-reporting-database/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ministry of Justice orders deletion of the UK&#x27;s largest court reporting database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>司法部下令删除英国最大法院报道数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47034713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 517</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47034713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 345</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47034713'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The UK Ministry of Justice has ordered the deletion of the country’s largest court reporting database, sparking concern over access to legal information and transparency. The move raises questions about data retention policies and the balance between privacy and public right to information. Legal communities worry this could hinder legal research and public scrutiny of judicial processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国司法部已下令删除该国最大的法院报道数据库，引发关于法律信息获取与透明度的担忧。此举引出了数据保留政策及隐私与公众知情权平衡的问题。法律界担心这将妨碍法律研究及公众对司法过程的监督。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.legalcheek.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/AdobeStock_110501092-1.jpeg' data-full='https://www.legalcheek.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/AdobeStock_110501092-1.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47142156' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/vinext/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How we rebuilt Next.js with AI in one week</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们用 AI 在一周内重建了 Next.js</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47142156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 515</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47142156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47142156'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A case study of rebuilding Next.js with AI assistance in a week, detailing tooling, workflow, and performance gains. The post argues AI can accelerate frontend framework iteration and feature delivery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文通过一个案例，讲述在一周内用 AI 辅助重建 Next.js 的过程、工具链和性能提升，展示 AI 如何加速前端框架的迭代与交付。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.hetzner.com/general/infrastructure-and-availability/price-adjustment/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hetzner Prices increase 30-40%</a></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47079222' data-tags='Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47079222' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/feb/19/trump-science-funding-cuts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We&#x27;re no longer attracting top talent: the brain drain killing American science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们不再吸引顶尖人才：美国科学的脑流失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47079222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 510</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47079222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 532</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47079222'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that US science is losing top talent due to funding and policy headwinds, risking long-term innovation. It points to structural levers — funding, visas, and research autonomy — to reverse the brain drain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为美国科学界正因资金与政策阻力流失顶尖人才，长期创新前景受损。文中提出通过资金、签证与研究自主权等结构性杠杆来扭转脑流失。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/a76381905d61e851c20bdcc942c03a3d1c09fcae/0_0_3000_2400/master/3000.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=33732a6ad1c54f625689635de2dc298a' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/a76381905d61e851c20bdcc942c03a3d1c09fcae/0_0_3000_2400/master/3000.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=33732a6ad1c54f625689635de2dc298a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47078324' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47078324' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kasava.dev/blog/ai-as-exoskeleton' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is not a coworker, it&#x27;s an exoskeleton</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 不是同事，而是外骨骼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47078324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 502</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47078324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 552</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47078324'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece reframes AI as augmentation rather than replacement, arguing that success hinges on designing workflows that leverage AI as an exoskeleton; includes practical guidance for teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章将 AI 视作增效工具而非替代品，强调通过将 AI 作为外骨骼来设计工作流程以提升协作效率，并给出团队落地的实用建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.kasava.dev/og-image.jpg' data-full='https://www.kasava.dev/og-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47197267' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47197267' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://help.obsidian.md/sync/headless' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Obsidian Sync now has a headless client</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Obsidian Sync 现已推出无头客户端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 501</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47197267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47197267'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Obsidian Sync now offers a headless client, enabling automation and UI-free integrations. Teams can script automated syncs, run schedules, and build custom tools around their notes. This broadens Obsidian into programmable knowledge management beyond personal note-taking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Obsidian Sync 現已推出無頭客戶端，方便自動化與無界面整合。團隊可透過腳本自動同步、排程，並打造與筆記相關的自訂工具，讓知識管理更具可程式化性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ogimage.obsidian.md/og-image.png?title=Headless+Sync&amp;description=Obsidian+Sync+offers+a+headless+client+to+sync+vaults+without+using+the+desktop+app.+Useful+for+CI+pipelines%2C+agents%2C+and+automated+workflows.+Sync+the+latest+changes+or+keep+files+continuously+up+to+date.&amp;logoUrl=https%3A%2F%2Fpublish-01.obsidian.md%2Faccess%2Ff786db9fac45774fa4f0d8112e232d67%2Ffavicon-96x96.png&amp;siteName=Obsidian+Help' data-full='https://ogimage.obsidian.md/og-image.png?title=Headless+Sync&amp;description=Obsidian+Sync+offers+a+headless+client+to+sync+vaults+without+using+the+desktop+app.+Useful+for+CI+pipelines%2C+agents%2C+and+automated+workflows.+Sync+the+latest+changes+or+keep+files+continuously+up+to+date.&amp;logoUrl=https%3A%2F%2Fpublish-01.obsidian.md%2Faccess%2Ff786db9fac45774fa4f0d8112e232d67%2Ffavicon-96x96.png&amp;siteName=Obsidian+Help' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://glashrvatske.hrt.hr/en/domestic/croatia-declared-free-of-landmines-after-31-years-12593533' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Croatia declared free of landmines after 31 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>克罗地亚在经过31年的清理后宣布无地雷区域</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47189535'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Croatia has been declared landmine-free after 31 years of clearance and monitoring, a milestone for safety and post-conflict recovery with implications for travel, commerce, and regional stability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>克罗地亚在经过长达31年的清理与监测后宣告无地雷区域，这是对安全与战后重建的重要里程碑，将影响旅游、贸易及区域稳定。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://milk.com/wall-o-shame/security_clearance.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What not to write on your security clearance form (1988)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在安保许可表格上不能写的内容（1988）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47102576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 495</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47102576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47102576'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retro piece highlights surprising or risky advice from a 1988 security clearance form, illustrating how bureaucratic processes shaped what applicants could disclose. It underscores the tension between honesty, risk, and career prospects in early tech hiring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾1988年的安保许可表填写建议，揭示繁琐的官僚流程如何影响求职者披露信息。文章强调诚实、风险与职业前景之间的张力，以及早期科技招聘中的不确定性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47160226' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47160226' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/02/tech-companies-shouldnt-be-bullied-doing-surveillance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tech companies shouldn&#x27;t be bullied into doing surveillance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技公司不该被迫参与监控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47160226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47160226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47160226'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EFF argues tech firms should resist being coerced into surveillance or data-sharing mandates. It calls for design and policy choices that protect privacy while enabling legitimate data-driven services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>电子前哨基金会主张科技公司不应被强迫参与监控或数据共享。文章强调在保护隐私的前提下，仍要支持合规的数据驱动服务的设计与政策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/ai-soldiers-3b.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/ai-soldiers-3b.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47154009' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47154009' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/02/final-2025-data-is-in-us-energy-use-is-up-as-solar-passes-hydro/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Following 35% growth, solar has passed hydro on US grid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>太阳能在美电网</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 484</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154009' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 456</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47154009'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New energy data shows solar generation surpassing hydro on the US grid after years of growth, signaling a major shift in the power mix. The change underscores the importance of storage, grid flexibility, and policy to support renewables.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新能源数据表明，太阳能在多年增长后超越水电，成为美国电网发电的主要来源之一。这一转变凸显储能、柔性电网和可再生能源相关政策的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-2255162141-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-2255162141-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47114530' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cia-factbook-archive.fly.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: CIA World Factbook Archive (1990–2025), searchable and exportable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：CIA World Factbook 档案（1990–2025），可搜索可导出</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47114530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 481</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47114530' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 99</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47114530'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new archive project collects the CIA World Factbook data from 1990 through 2025, making it searchable and exportable. The dataset supports longitudinal geopolitical research and tool-building for data scientists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项档案项目整理了自1990年至2025年的 CIA 世界概况数据，使其可搜索并导出。该数据集便于进行长期地缘政治研究和数据科学工具开发。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47045804' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.arthurcnops.blog/death-of-show-hn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is Show HN dead? No, but it&#x27;s drowning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN 死了吗？不，但正陷入困境</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47045804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 478</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47045804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 399</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47045804'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The popular hacker community feature Show HN is not dead but is experiencing declining engagement and visibility. Factors include increased noise, changing user behavior, and platform evolution making it harder for quality projects to stand out. This signals challenges for niche tech showcases in maintaining relevance amid growing content saturation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>知名黑客社区栏目Show HN虽然未死，但参与度和曝光率正在下降。原因包括信息过载、用户行为改变及平台演进导致优质项目难以脱颖而出。这反映出技术小众展示在内容饱和中保持影响力的挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47057824' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47057824' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gist.github.com/jake-stewart/0a8ea46159a7da2c808e5be2177e1783' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Terminals should generate the 256-color palette</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>终端应生成 256 色调色板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47057824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 476</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47057824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47057824'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposal argues terminals should programmatically generate the full 256-color palette instead of relying on fixed mappings, improving color consistency across emulators and applications. Generating the palette at runtime would reduce surprises from differing defaults and make color-aware tooling and themes more portable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该提议主张终端应在运行时生成完整的 256 色调色板，而不是依赖固定映射，以提升不同模拟器与应用间的颜色一致性。运行时生成色板能减少因默认设置不同产生的意外，使颜色主题和工具更易迁移。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' data-full='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A four-year retrospective on infra decisions highlights what worked and what didn’t, with lessons on observability, resilience, cost management, and vendor diversification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对四年基础设施决策的回顾，揭示哪些做法有效，哪些带来后悔，并给出在可观测性、韧性、成本管理和供应商多元化等方面的经验教训。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ben-evans.com/benedictevans/2026/2/19/how-will-openai-compete-nkg2x' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How will OpenAI compete?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 如何竞争？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47158975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 470</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47158975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 647</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47158975'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ben Evans analyzes how OpenAI could compete: with open ecosystems, strategic partnerships, and differentiating offerings beyond core models. The piece lays out plausible paths for OpenAI to sustain advantage in a crowded AI market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>贝恩·埃文斯分析 OpenAI 的竞争路径，聚焦开放生态、战略伙伴关系以及对核心模型之外的差异化能力。提出在竞争激烈的 AI 市场中维持优势的可行路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/50363cf324ac8e905e7df861/t/6993b59b8517240d2235240a/1771507646532/Frontier%2BLanguage%2BModel%2BIntelligence%2C%2BOver%2BTime%2B%2824%2BJan%2B%2726%29%2B-2.png.webp?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/50363cf324ac8e905e7df861/t/6993b59b8517240d2235240a/1771507646532/Frontier%2BLanguage%2BModel%2BIntelligence%2C%2BOver%2BTime%2B%2824%2BJan%2B%2726%29%2B-2.png.webp?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>認知債務：當速度超過理解力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47196582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 469</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47196582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 205</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47196582'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that pushing development velocity too hard creates cognitive debt—hidden mental load and architectural debt that slows future work. It promotes better onboarding, documentation, and mental models to keep velocity sustainable. The takeaway: speed must be matched with clarity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章認為過度追求開發速度會產生認知債務，即藏匿的心理負擔與架構負擔，最終拖慢未來的工作。建議透過更好的上手指引、文件與心智模型，讓速度持續可行。核心觀點是：速度需要與清晰度並行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.rockoder.com/images/id/id.png' data-full='https://www.rockoder.com/images/id/id.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47183578' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47183578' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dignitymemorial.com/obituaries/longmont-co/daniel-simmons-12758871' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dan Simmons, author of Hyperion, has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《Hyperion》作者丹·西蒙斯去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47183578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 468</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47183578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47183578'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dan Simmons, author of Hyperion, has died, marking the loss of a beloved sci-fi voice known for intricate cosmology and richly crafted worlds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科幻作家《Hyperion》作者丹·西蒙斯去世，因其错综复杂的宇宙观和丰富的世界观而深受读者喜爱。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d3eguztg5751m.cloudfront.net/as/assets-mem-com/cmi/1/7/8/8/12758871/20260226_130858076_0_orig.jpg/-/daniel-simmons-longmont-co-obituary.jpg' data-full='https://d3eguztg5751m.cloudfront.net/as/assets-mem-com/cmi/1/7/8/8/12758871/20260226_130858076_0_orig.jpg/-/daniel-simmons-longmont-co-obituary.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47184288' data-tags='Career,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47184288' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pseudosingleton.com/leaving-google-improved-my-life/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Leaving Google has actively improved my life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>离开谷歌后我的生活明显改善</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47184288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 467</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47184288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 258</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47184288'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal essay argues that leaving Google dramatically improved the author&#x27;s life, citing autonomy, privacy, and focus gains. The piece adds to ongoing debates about workplace culture, tech giants, and personal freedom.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在个人随笔中表示离开谷歌后生活明显改善，强调自主性、隐私和专注度的提升。此文为职场文化与科技巨头对个人自由影响的讨论再添讨论点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/herman-1683556668-0.png' data-full='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/herman-1683556668-0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47063005' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47063005' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tailscale.com/blog/peer-relays-ga' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tailscale Peer Relays is now generally available</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tailscale Peer Relays 现已全面可用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47063005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 464</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47063005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47063005'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tailscale announced general availability of Peer Relays, a feature that simplifies NAT traversal by relaying traffic between peers through trusted nodes when direct connections fail. This improves connectivity reliability for devices behind restrictive networks and reduces manual relay setup for teams using private mesh VPNs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tailscale 宣布 Peer Relays 功能已全面可用，该功能在点对点直接连接失败时通过受信任节点中继流量，简化 NAT 穿透。它提升了处于受限网络设备的连接可靠性，并减少了团队在私有网格 VPN 中手动设置中继的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/w77i7m8x/production/ccbb854e716b6a58e62ccb0fd3eed23c2bf77599-2304x1187.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/w77i7m8x/production/ccbb854e716b6a58e62ccb0fd3eed23c2bf77599-2304x1187.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47064490' data-tags='Data,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47064490' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jasonfantl.com/posts/Universal-Unique-IDs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cosmologically Unique IDs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宇宙学唯一 ID</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47064490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 458</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47064490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 150</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47064490'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author proposes a scheme for universally unique identifiers based on cosmological timestamps or other large-scale physical references to reduce collision probability without centralized coordination. Such ideas aim to make IDs traceable to an external entropy source, though practical deployment would need interoperability agreements and consideration of privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者提出一种基于宇宙学时间戳或大尺度物理参考的全局唯一标识方案，试图在无需中心化协调下尽量降低冲突概率。此类构想将 ID 与外部熵源关联，具有可追溯性，但实际部署需考虑互操作协议和隐私问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jasonfantl.com/https:/blog-assets.jasonemerald.workers.dev/CUIDs/cover.png' data-full='https://jasonfantl.com/https:/blog-assets.jasonemerald.workers.dev/CUIDs/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47051852' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy,Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47051852' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asteroidos.org/news/2-0-release/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: AsteroidOS 2.0 – Nobody asked, we shipped anyway</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：AsteroidOS 2.0 — 没人要求，但我们还是发布了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47051852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 457</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47051852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47051852'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AsteroidOS 2.0 is a new release of the open-source smartwatch OS, adding features and improvements for privacy-first wearable users. The update showcases active community development and provides an alternative for users who want a Linux-based, customizable smartwatch platform without vendor lock-in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AsteroidOS 2.0 发布，为开源智能手表操作系统带来新功能和改进，面向重视隐私的可穿戴设备用户。此更新显示社区仍在积极开发，提供了一个基于 Linux、可高度定制且无厂商锁定的替代选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asteroidos.org/public/img/logo.png' data-full='https://asteroidos.org/public/img/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47051546' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47051546' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/02/17/tesla-robotaxi-adds-5-more-crashes-austin-month-4x-worse-than-humans/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla &#x27;Robotaxi&#x27; adds 5 more crashes in Austin in a month – 4x worse than humans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉“Robotaxi”在奥斯汀一个月内又添 5 起事故——比人类差 4 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47051546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 455</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47051546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47051546'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reported cluster of crashes involving Tesla&#x27;s Robotaxi service in Austin added five incidents in a month, with claims the autonomous fleet is crashing at higher rates than human drivers. If accurate, the pattern raises questions about deployment readiness and the validity of claims that robotaxis are safer than human-operated vehicles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称特斯拉在奥斯汀的 Robotaxi 服务一个月内又发生五起事故，有报道称该自动车队的事故率高于人类司机。若属实，这一趋势将对自动驾驶投放成熟度及其比人类更安全的论断提出质疑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2021/08/Tesla-Full-Self-Driving-Beta-Hero.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2021/08/Tesla-Full-Self-Driving-Beta-Hero.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47133313' data-tags='AI,Security,Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47133313' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://serverhost.com/blog/firefox-148-launches-with-exciting-ai-kill-switch-feature-and-more-enhancements/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Firefox 148 Launches with AI Kill Switch Feature and More Enhancements</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Firefox 148 发布，新增 AI 取消开关等多项增强</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47133313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 454</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47133313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 383</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47133313'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 148 debuts with an &#x27;AI Kill Switch&#x27; to disable AI features, addressing user control and privacy concerns in browsing helpers. The release also brings several performance and privacy enhancements, reflecting Mozilla&#x27;s stance on user autonomy in an AI-enabled browser.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 148 引入“AI 取消开关”，让用户在浏览器中的 AI 功能可控，回应隐私与信任问题。同时还带来多项性能与隐私改进，体现 Mozilla 在 AI 圈定用户自主权的立场。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47052941' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47052941' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Zaneham/BarraCUDA' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BarraCUDA Open-source CUDA compiler targeting AMD GPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BarraCUDA：面向 AMD GPU 的开源 CUDA 编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47052941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 450</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47052941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47052941'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BarraCUDA is an open-source project that compiles CUDA code to run on AMD GPUs, aiming to bridge NVIDIA-centric CUDA ecosystems and AMD hardware. It could help developers reuse CUDA workloads on non-NVIDIA devices, lowering friction for heterogeneous GPU environments, though compatibility and performance trade-offs will determine adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BarraCUDA 是个开源项目，目标是把 CUDA 代码编译到 AMD GPU 上，试图缩小以 NVIDIA 为中心的 CUDA 生态与 AMD 硬件之间的差距。它能让开发者在非 NVIDIA 设备上复用 CUDA 工作负载，但兼容性和性能权衡将决定其采用程度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1535270acea945dd40b2bf5d95a8cce2750674805a562806b85adc7e23be1f1a/Zaneham/BarraCUDA' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1535270acea945dd40b2bf5d95a8cce2750674805a562806b85adc7e23be1f1a/Zaneham/BarraCUDA' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47094768' data-tags='Hardware,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47094768' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.withdiode.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Diode – Build, program, and simulate hardware</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Diode：构建、编程与仿真硬件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47094768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 447</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47094768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47094768'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Diode offers an end-to-end platform for hardware development, letting you design circuits, write firmware, and run simulations in one place. By unifying hardware prototyping steps, it reduces onboarding friction and accelerates experimentation for hobbyists and teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Diode 提供一个端到端的硬件开发平台，集成电路设计、固件编写与仿真。通过统一原型开发流程，降低入门门槛、加速迭代，适合爱好者和团队使用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://withdiode.com/og-image.png' data-full='https://withdiode.com/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47066241' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47066241' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/MacOS/comments/1r8900z/macos_which_officially_supports_27_year_old/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>27-year-old Apple iBooks can connect to Wi-Fi and download official updates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>27 年前的 Apple iBooks 能连 Wi‑Fi 并下载官方更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47066241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 446</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47066241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47066241'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An old Apple iBook from 1999 was able to connect to Wi‑Fi and receive official updates, demonstrating surprising longevity in legacy hardware and protocol compatibility. The find highlights how long-lived standards and minimal server-side compatibility can keep vintage devices functional in modern networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一台 1999 年的 Apple iBook 能够连接 Wi‑Fi 并下载官方更新，显示出旧硬件在协议兼容性方面的惊人持久性。这个发现强调了长期存在的标准和服务器端的向后兼容性如何让老设备在现代网络中继续可用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://keepandroidopen.org/open-letter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open Letter to Google on Mandatory Developer Registration for App Distribution</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A public letter urges Google to rethink mandatory developer registration for app distribution, arguing it could hamper innovation, transparency, and developer autonomy. The authors advocate more open, privacy-conscious approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公开信呼吁 Google 重新考虑应用分发的强制开发者注册，指其可能削弱创新与开发者自治，主张更开放、隐私友好的分发方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/02/24/discord-peter-thiel-backed-persona-identity-verification-breach/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Discord cuts ties with identity verification software, Persona</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Discord 终止使用 Persona 的身份验证服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 438</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 316</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47136036'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discord ends its use of Persona&#x27;s identity verification after concerns over privacy and security, altering how users verify accounts. The move highlights the fragility of third-party identity services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Discord 终止使用 Persona 的身份验证服务，影响用户验证流程；事件凸显了第三方身份服务的脆弱性和隐私安全挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asahilinux.org/2026/02/progress-report-6-19/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asahi Linux Progress Report: Linux 6.19</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Asahi Linux 进展报告：Linux 6.19</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47059275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 437</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47059275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47059275'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Asahi Linux team reports progress toward upstreaming support for Linux 6.19 on Apple Silicon, detailing driver work, performance fixes, and remaining hardware compatibility tasks. The update signals steady advancement in making mainline kernels usable on newer Mac hardware, reducing the gap for Linux users on Apple devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux 团队汇报了在 Apple Silicon 上支持 Linux 6.19 的进展，介绍了驱动开发、性能修复以及尚需解决的硬件兼容任务。该更新表明在使主线内核可用于新 Mac 硬件方面正在稳步推进，逐步缩小 Apple 设备上运行 Linux 的差距。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medicalxpress.com/news/2026-02-blood-boosts-alzheimer-diagnosis-accuracy.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blood test boosts Alzheimer&#x27;s diagnosis accuracy to 94.5%, clinical study shows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>血液检测将阿尔茨海默病诊断准确率提升至94.5%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47132388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 434</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47132388' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47132388'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clinical study shows a blood test achieving 94.5% accuracy for Alzheimer&#x27;s diagnosis, potentially enabling earlier and less invasive screening. While promising, questions remain about validation across diverse populations and integration into standard care.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项临床研究显示，一种血液检测能将阿尔茨海默病诊断准确率提升至94.5%，有望实现更早且无创的筛查。尽管前景乐观，仍需在不同人群中的验证以及在临床常规中的落地应用等问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/blood-test-boosts-alzh.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/blood-test-boosts-alzh.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47193064' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mksg.lu/blog/context-mode' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MCP server that reduces Claude Code context consumption by 98%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>將 Claude 的上下文消耗降低 98% 的 MCP 伺服器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47193064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 430</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47193064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47193064'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A server-side optimization (MCP) reduces Claude Code&#x27;s context window usage by about 98%, enabling longer prompts and lower costs on resource-constrained setups. This points to a path for running larger models locally with tighter budgets. Expect bigger prompts and less cloud reliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一項服務端優化（MCP）使 Claude Code 的上下文消耗降低約 98%，在資源有限的環境中可支援更長的提示與較低成本。這為在本地以較小預算運行更大模型提供了一條可能路徑，預期未來會有更長的上下文與更少的雲端依賴。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The post showcases technical milestones like improved compatibility and performance optimizations. It reflects the vibrant open-source community dedicated to preserving classic gaming experiences through advanced emulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《三角力量的崛起》是Dolphin模拟器的最新进展报告，介绍了GameCube和Wii游戏模拟方面的重大成就与未来规划。文中展示了兼容性提升和性能优化等技术里程碑，体现了开放源代码社区通过先进模拟技术保护经典游戏的活力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47129361' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47129361' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vladimir.varank.in/notes/2026/02/freebsd-brcmfmac/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FreeBSD doesn&#x27;t have Wi-Fi driver for my old MacBook, so AI built one for me</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 为我的老款 MacBook 在 FreeBSD 上自动生成了 Wi‑Fi 驱动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47129361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 427</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47129361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 356</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47129361'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI-assisted workflow generated a working Wi-Fi driver for a legacy MacBook under FreeBSD, filling a long-standing hardware support gap. The example showcases how AI can accelerate OS hobbyist projects and extend older hardware lifecycles, though it raises questions about driver reliability and licensing considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 帮助下，FreeBSD 为一台老旧 MacBook 自动生成了兼容的 Wi‑Fi 驱动，填补长期的硬件支持空白。此案例展示了 AI 在操作系统爱好者项目中的作用，能延长旧硬件的使用寿命，但也引发关于驱动可靠性与许可的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vladimir.varank.in/v.jpg' data-full='https://vladimir.varank.in/v.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47124171'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MIT&#x27;s Missing Semester gets a 2026 refresh, updating tooling, workflows, and practical skills for students to bridge theory and real-world system work. The revision emphasizes hands-on system knowledge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MIT 的 Missing Semester 更新至 2026 版，聚焦工具链、工作流与面向真实系统的实践技能，帮助学生把理论与实际工作联系起来。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://harpers.org/archive/2026/03/childs-play-sam-kriss-ai-startup-roy-lee/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Child&#x27;s Play: Tech&#x27;s new generation and the end of thinking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技的儿童化：新一代与思考能力的终结</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47088685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 424</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47088685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 258</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47088685'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of how tech and startup culture may encourage passive consumption and erode critical thinking. It argues for preserving human judgment in design and development to avoid over-reliance on automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评科技与初创文化可能让新一代陷入被动使用、削弱批判性思维。主张在设计与开发中保留人类判断，避免对自动化的过度依赖。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47049479' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://go.dev/blog/gofix' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using go fix to modernize Go code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 go fix 现代化 Go 代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 424</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47049479'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide explains how to use go fix to automatically apply idiomatic modernizations and migration changes to Go codebases, easing upgrades to newer language versions. It walks through common transforms and cautions on review and testing, highlighting go fix as a practical tool for large-scale refactors with minimal manual edits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇指南介绍如何使用 go fix 自动对 Go 代码库进行符合现代风格和版本迁移的改造，从而简化升级到新语言版本的工作。文中列举常见的转换并提醒要注意代码审查与测试，强调 go fix 是大规模重构时减少手工修改的实用工具。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47072968'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Paged Out 第8期汇聚了围绕科技文化、设计与未来交互的随笔和艺术作品，适合欣赏独立声线的读者。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/yjeanrenaud/yj_nearbyglasses' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nearby Glasses</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nearby Glasses 开源原型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47140042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 420</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47140042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 202</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47140042'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nearby Glasses is an open-source AR glasses project enabling contextual information about nearby people or places, illustrating how lightweight hardware and vision stacks can enable social AR.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nearby Glasses 是一个开源的增强现实眼镜项目，提供对周边人和地点的上下文信息，展示了轻量硬件与视觉栈在社交 AR 领域的应用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47050067' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gentoo.org/news/2026/02/16/codeberg.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gentoo on Codeberg</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gentoo 登陆 Codeberg</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47050067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 419</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47050067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47050067'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gentoo announced hosting some of its repositories and infrastructure on Codeberg, signaling a move toward decentralized or alternative code hosting options. The shift reflects broader interest in diversifying hosting providers and aligning with communities favoring open-source-friendly, European-based platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gentoo 宣布将其部分仓库与基础设施迁至 Codeberg，表明其在寻求更分散或替代的代码托管选项。此举反映了在多样化托管服务商以及倾向于开源友好、欧洲平台的社区中日益增长的兴趣。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gentoo.org/assets/img/og-image.jpg' data-full='https://www.gentoo.org/assets/img/og-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47153798' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://worksinprogress.co/issue/the-united-states-needs-fewer-bus-stops/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bus stop balancing is fast, cheap, and effective</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>公交站点再平衡：快速、廉价且有效</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47153798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 419</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47153798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 625</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47153798'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposal that reducing bus stops can make urban bus networks faster, cheaper, and more efficient. The approach challenges conventional wisdom about serving everywhere and suggests smarter routing and stop placement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过精简公交停靠点可以让公交网络更快、更省钱且更高效。这一做法挑战以往“全覆盖”的思路，强调更智能的路线与停靠点布局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://worksinprogress.co/wip-image/uploads/2026/01/Issue-19-share-cards-55.png' data-full='https://worksinprogress.co/wip-image/uploads/2026/01/Issue-19-share-cards-55.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stackoverflow.com/questions/818255/what-does-21-mean' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What does &quot; 2&gt;&amp;1 &quot; mean?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>&quot;2&gt;&amp;1&quot; 的含义是什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47171233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 415</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47171233' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47171233'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>&quot;2&gt;&amp;1&quot; merges standard error and output streams in shells, a fundamental redirection technique. It covers common use cases, caveats, and how it behaves in pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>&quot;2&gt;&amp;1&quot; 将标准错误输出合并到标准输出，常用于日志和管道中的输出控制。说明了基本用法、常见场景以及潜在的坑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stackoverflow.com/Content/Sites/stackoverflow/Img/apple-touch-icon@2.png?v=0f0cab681579' data-full='https://stackoverflow.com/Content/Sites/stackoverflow/Img/apple-touch-icon@2.png?v=0f0cab681579' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
