</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47104973' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47104973' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dbreunig.com/2026/02/21/why-is-claude-an-electron-app.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is Claude an Electron app?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 Claude 是一个 Electron 应用？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47104973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 414</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47104973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 441</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47104973'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues about the trade-offs of packaging Claude as an Electron app, balancing cross-platform ease and performance costs. It explains how app size, memory usage, and update cadence affect user experience and developer choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文讨论将 Claude 作为 Electron 应用打包的取舍，权衡跨平台便利性与性能成本。应用体积、内存占用和更新节奏等因素如何影响用户体验与开发者选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.dbreunig.com/img/sf_beach_og.jpg' data-full='https://www.dbreunig.com/img/sf_beach_og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47111400' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47111400' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.popsci.com/technology/robot-vacuum-army/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Man accidentally gains control of 7k robot vacuums</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>男子意外控制了 7,000 台机器人吸尘器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 413</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47111400'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security misconfiguration allowed one person to control around 7,000 robot vacuums, exposing IoT risk at scale. The incident underscores why strong authentication, firmware hardening, and vendor safeguards matter for consumer devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次安全配置错误让一人能够控制约7000台机器人吸尘器，暴露了大规模物联网风险。事件强调了强认证、固件加固和厂商防护对消费级设备的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.popsci.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/robot_army.jpg?quality=85&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://www.popsci.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/robot_army.jpg?quality=85&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47167763' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47167763' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ndss-symposium.org/wp-content/uploads/2026-f1282-paper.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AirSnitch: Demystifying and breaking client isolation in Wi-Fi networks [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AirSnitch：揭示并突破 Wi-Fi 网络中的客户端隔离</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 407</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47167763'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AirSnitch demonstrates practical methods to defeat client isolation in Wi‑Fi networks, revealing attack vectors. The work motivates stronger AP isolation and mitigations, underscoring ongoing security concerns in wireless deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AirSnitch 的研究揭示了 Wi‑Fi 客户端隔离的可被利用的缺口，指出了改进接入点隔离和其他缓解措施的必要性。对无线网络安全的警示意义明显。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47042136' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47042136' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/ai-is-destroying-open-source/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is destroying open source, and it&#x27;s not even good yet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI正在摧毁开源，且还远不成熟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47042136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47042136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 332</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47042136'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The rapid influx of AI-generated content is disrupting open source ecosystems, often flooding repositories with low-quality or redundant code. Despite AI’s potential, current implementations risk undermining community standards, maintainability, and collaboration culture. This warns that harnessing AI in open source requires careful curation and new governance models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI生成内容的快速涌入扰乱了开源生态，常导致大量低质量或重复代码充斥仓库。尽管AI具潜力，但现有应用风险削弱社区标准、维护性和协作氛围。提醒我们要在开源中利用AI，必须审慎管理和创新治理模式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47103661' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47103661' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anuragk.com/blog/posts/Taalas.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Taalas “prints” LLM onto a chip?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Taalas 如何把 LLM 印到芯片上？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47103661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47103661' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47103661'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Taalas demonstrates an approach to mapping or embedding LLM capabilities into hardware, detailing hardware-software co-design techniques to run models on limited silicon. The piece illustrates the ongoing trend of on-chip AI efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Taalas 展示了将 LLM 能力映射或嵌入到硬件中的方法，介绍了在有限硬件上运行模型的软硬件协同设计思路，体现了芯片级 AI 高效化的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.anuragk.com/blog/staticFiles/Taalas/GPUInference.png' data-full='https://www.anuragk.com/blog/staticFiles/Taalas/GPUInference.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47047010' data-tags='Open Source,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47047010' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.freecad.org/index.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FreeCAD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FreeCAD：开源参数化 CAD 工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47047010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 403</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47047010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47047010'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FreeCAD is an open-source, parametric 3D CAD tool with extensible architecture and scripting. It’s popular for hobbyists and engineers alike, offering customizable workflows for mechanical design and hobby projects without licensing fees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FreeCAD 是一款开源的参数化 3D CAD 工具，具备可扩展的架构与脚本化能力。它在爱好者与工程师之间都很受欢迎，提供可自定义的工作流用于机械设计与原型开发，且无许可费。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://freecad.org/images/banner.jpg' data-full='https://freecad.org/images/banner.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47199781' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47199781' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://venturebeat.com/technology/alibabas-new-open-source-qwen3-5-medium-models-offer-sonnet-4-5-performance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen3.5 122B and 35B models offer Sonnet 4.5 performance on local computers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen3.5 122B 与 35B 模型在本地电脑上达到 Sonnet 4.5 性能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47199781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 401</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47199781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47199781'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen3.5 family offers 122B and 35B variants that match Sonnet 4.5 performance on local hardware, signaling strong open-source on-device LLM capabilities. This narrows the gap to cloud services and fuels offline AI exploration, though licensing and ecosystem support remain considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿里巴巴的 Qwen3.5 家族提供 122B 与 35B 版本，在本地硬件上达到 Sonnet 4.5 的性能水平，显示開源大模型在本地推理方面的強大能力。這縮小了與雲端服務的差距，促進離線 AI 的探索，但授權與生態系統支援仍需留意。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47075455' data-tags='Hardware,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47075455' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/solid-state-lidar-microvision-adas' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sub-$200 Lidar could reshuffle auto sensor economics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不足200美元的激光雷达或将重塑汽车传感器经济</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47075455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 401</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47075455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 537</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47075455'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cheaper sub-$200 solid-state lidar could reshape the economics of automotive sensing, lowering per-vehicle sensor costs and accelerating ADAS adoption. But manufacturers still face hurdles in performance, reliability, and scalable production that will determine how quickly the market shifts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>成本低廉的固态激光雷达可能重塑汽车传感器的经济格局，显著降低单车传感器成本并推动高级驾驶辅助系统的普及。然而要实现大规模生产，性能、可靠性与产能扩张仍是关键挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=64423665&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C330%2C0%2C170' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=64423665&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C330%2C0%2C170' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47167931' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47167931' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://read.technically.dev/p/vibe-coding-and-the-maker-movement' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Will vibe coding end like the maker movement?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vibe 编码会像创客运动一样终结吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 399</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47167931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 424</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47167931'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vibe coding is gaining attention, but questions remain about its long-term value beyond hype and maker culture nostalgia. The piece weighs whether a culture-driven approach to programming can scale or endure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vibe 编码的热潮引发对其长期价值的质疑：是否只是一时的炒作，还是能否支撑可持续的开发文化与工具生态？</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!BCv8!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F930faad1-6c70-4d57-a2f1-5273483daa28_644x447.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!BCv8!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F930faad1-6c70-4d57-a2f1-5273483daa28_644x447.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47119274' data-tags='Science,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47119274' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magicalmushroom.com/index' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Magical Mushroom – Europe&#x27;s first industrial-scale mycelium packaging producer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Magical Mushroom：欧洲首家工业规模菌丝体包装生产商</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47119274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 385</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47119274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47119274'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe&#x27;s first industrial-scale mycelium packaging producer aims to replace plastics with fungal-based materials. The move signals growing interest in biodegradable, low-carbon packaging, though commercialization, performance, and supply-chain hurdles remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲首家以菌丝体为材料的工业级包装生产商正推动用真菌材料替代塑料包装，强调可降解、低碳环保的潜力。尽管市场前景乐观，规模化生产、性能表现和供应链仍面临挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://a.storyblok.com/f/142449/2500x1667/4cab6ab4ac/lm0_9542_rt.jpg' data-full='https://a.storyblok.com/f/142449/2500x1667/4cab6ab4ac/lm0_9542_rt.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47161160' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47161160' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/02/ram-now-represents-35-percent-of-bill-of-materials-for-hp-pcs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RAM now represents 35 percent of bill of materials for HP PCs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RAM 已占 HP PC 物料成本的 35%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47161160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 381</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47161160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 335</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47161160'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RAM now accounts for 35% of HP PC BOM, reflecting rising memory costs and demand; this squeezes margins and may push design choices toward more cost-effective memory configurations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RAM 现已占据 HP PC 物料成本的约 35%，反映出内存成本上升与需求强劲。这可能压缩利润率并驱动向更具性价比的内存配置调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-2252687789-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-2252687789-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47104667' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47104667' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/xaskasdf/ntransformer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Llama 3.1 70B on a single RTX 3090 via NVMe-to-GPU bypassing the CPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：通过 NVMe‑to‑GPU 绕过 CPU 在单张 RTX 3090 上跑 Llama 3.1 70B</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47104667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 381</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47104667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47104667'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community post shows a technique to run a 70B-parameter Llama model on a single RTX 3090 by streaming weights from NVMe directly to GPU memory, bypassing CPU bottlenecks. It demonstrates how clever data pipelines can unlock consumer hardware for large models, with trade-offs in latency and stability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了一种通过直接从 NVMe 将权重流式传输到 GPU 内存来在单张 RTX 3090 上运行 70B 参数 Llama 的方法，绕过 CPU 瓶颈。此举证明了巧妙的数据管线能够让消费级硬件也能运行大型模型，但在延迟和稳定性方面存在取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f349a6f682984a4f895b65fb8bba8433fb9bb844b7be4abfb2b3bcaf022da83e/xaskasdf/ntransformer' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f349a6f682984a4f895b65fb8bba8433fb9bb844b7be4abfb2b3bcaf022da83e/xaskasdf/ntransformer' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47062748' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47062748' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chromereleases.googleblog.com/2026/02/stable-channel-update-for-desktop_13.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zero-day CSS: CVE-2026-2441 exists in the wild</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>零日 CSS：CVE-2026-2441 已在野外利用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47062748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 375</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47062748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47062748'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A zero-day vulnerability identified as CVE-2026-2441 affects CSS processing and is reportedly being exploited in the wild, prompting urgent browser updates. The issue underscores that presentation-layer bugs can have real security impact and that prompt patching of browsers is critical for users and administrators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个编号为 CVE-2026-2441 的零日漏洞影响 CSS 解析，并被报道已在野外被利用，促使浏览器进行紧急更新。此事提醒我们表现层的缺陷也能带来实际安全风险，用户和管理员应及时打补丁。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEjP1xLDmFLSwhDBm3s-ybDpo-Wh_uM2bjqmbh5PiX-svixf-idIIDE-Nd6oLSHvvnpAql9hyPKBtK1zSufmg8RS1g16YmCZbbzPMhw72S7Dv7JBojpNzzMj5qDQtkAewk8K_r3mrRSGiPZs/s1600/googlelogo_color_200x200.png' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEjP1xLDmFLSwhDBm3s-ybDpo-Wh_uM2bjqmbh5PiX-svixf-idIIDE-Nd6oLSHvvnpAql9hyPKBtK1zSufmg8RS1g16YmCZbbzPMhw72S7Dv7JBojpNzzMj5qDQtkAewk8K_r3mrRSGiPZs/s1600/googlelogo_color_200x200.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47096253' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47096253' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/karpathy/status/2024987174077432126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claws are now a new layer on top of LLM agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claws 成为大语言模型代理的新层</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 839</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47096253'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claws adds a new layer on top of LLM agents to improve safety, governance, and composability of autonomous tasks. It aims to provide a modular framework that handles orchestration, policy enforcement, and error handling beyond a single prompt. This could advance reliable agent AI, though adoption details remain to be seen.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claws 在大语言模型代理之上引入新的一层，提升自治任务的安全性、治理与模块化能力，提供用于编排、策略执行和错误处理的分层框架。此举有望推动更可靠的代理式 AI，但具体落地与普及情况尚待观察。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1296667294148382721/9Pr6XrPB_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1296667294148382721/9Pr6XrPB_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47035679' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47035679' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rockpapershotgun.com/good-news-uk-discord-users-were-part-of-a-peter-thiel-linked-data-collection-experiment' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UK Discord users were part of a Peter Thiel-linked data collection experiment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国Discord用户参与了彼得·蒂尔相关数据采集实验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47035679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47035679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47035679'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UK Discord users were unknowingly involved in a data collection experiment linked to investor Peter Thiel, raising privacy and ethical concerns. This revelation spotlights the growing use of popular social platforms for covert research or data harvesting. It calls attention to the need for clearer consent and transparency in digital data practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国Discord用户在不知情的情况下参与了与投资人彼得·蒂尔相关的数据采集实验，引发隐私和伦理争议。这一揭露凸显了流行社交平台被用于隐秘研究或数据收集的趋势，强调数字数据操作中需加强明确同意和透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assetsio.gnwcdn.com/Discordageverification2.png?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;fit=crop&amp;enable=upscale&amp;auto=webp' data-full='https://assetsio.gnwcdn.com/Discordageverification2.png?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;fit=crop&amp;enable=upscale&amp;auto=webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47034192' data-tags='Hardware,AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47034192' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mashable.com/article/ai-hard-drive-hdd-shortages-western-digital-sold-out' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thanks a lot, AI: Hard drives are sold out for the year, says WD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>感谢AI：西部数据称硬盘全年售罄</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47034192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 373</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47034192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 306</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47034192'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Western Digital announced that hard drives are sold out for the year, largely due to increased demand driven by AI workloads and data centers. This shortage reflects broader supply-demand imbalances as AI infrastructure scales rapidly. It highlights how AI growth can stress traditional hardware supply chains in unexpected ways.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西部数据表示硬盘全年已售罄，主要因AI工作负载和数据中心需求激增。此短缺反映出随着AI基础设施快速扩展，供需失衡加剧，显示AI发展对传统硬件供应链带来意想不到的压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://helios-i.mashable.com/imagery/articles/03BMp5tylVs9DJJavYCVFKV/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.v1771180235.jpg' data-full='https://helios-i.mashable.com/imagery/articles/03BMp5tylVs9DJJavYCVFKV/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.v1771180235.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47125349' data-tags='Hardware,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47125349' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/china/asml-unveils-euv-light-source-advance-that-could-yield-50-more-chips-by-2030-2026-02-23/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ASML unveils EUV light source advance that could yield 50% more chips by 2030</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ASML 公布 EUV 光源的进展，到2030年或使芯片产量提升50%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 368</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47125349'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ASML reveals a next-generation EUV light source that could boost chip yields by up to 50% by 2030, easing the roadmap pressure for leading-node fabrication. If realized, this could lower costs and extend Moore&#x27;s law-like trajectories, though integration into fabs will take years.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ASML 公布下一代 EUV 光源技术，若商用可实现到2030年产出提升约50%，将缓解先进制程的产能压力并推动成本下降。实现仍需时间，在实际 fabs 的整合也会面临挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47136611' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47136611' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hacks.mozilla.org/2026/02/goodbye-innerhtml-hello-sethtml-stronger-xss-protection-in-firefox-148/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Goodbye InnerHTML, Hello SetHTML: Stronger XSS Protection in Firefox 148</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>告别 InnerHTML，迎来 SetHTML：Firefox 148 提升 XSS 防护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 366</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47136611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47136611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 148 introduces SetHTML for safer handling of HTML to reduce XSS risks, moving away from innerHTML in many contexts. Developers are advised on updating code to use safer APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 148 引入 SetHTML，提升对 HTML 的安全处理，降低 XSS 风险，逐步替代众多场景中的 innerHTML；开发者需要改用更安全的 API。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hacks.mozilla.org/wp-content/themes/Hax/img/hacks-meta-image.jpg' data-full='https://hacks.mozilla.org/wp-content/themes/Hax/img/hacks-meta-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47049573'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HackMyClaw appears to be a hardware-hacking or modding project focused on Claw machines (or a specific device named &#x27;Claw&#x27;), offering guides, tools, or a community for tinkering. Such projects typically aim to reverse-engineer mechanics, add remote control, or create novel interactions for hobbyist experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HackMyClaw 似乎是一个针对抓娃娃机（或名为“Claw”的设备）的硬件改造项目，提供改装指南、工具或交流社区。此类项目通常致力于逆向机械结构、添加远程控制或为爱好者创造新玩法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47125374' data-tags='AI,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47125374' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/guides/agentic-engineering-patterns/code-is-cheap/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Writing code is cheap now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>写代码现在很便宜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 362</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47125374' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 467</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47125374'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cheap code is accelerating prototyping and experimentation as AI copilots handle boilerplate and refactoring. The piece argues this lowers barriers for building, but warns that maintainability and security still require strong design — new patterns like agentic engineering emerge to manage complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着 AI 助手接手大量模板化与重复性编码，写代码成本显著降低，开发速度加快。文章提醒人们，尽管成本下降，代码质量、维护性和安全性仍需依赖良好设计，并提出将代理式工程等新模式用于管理复杂性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47128138' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47128138' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opper.ai/blog/car-wash-test' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Car Wash” test with 53 models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“Car Wash”测试覆盖53种模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47128138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 361</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47128138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 433</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47128138'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The &#x27;Car Wash&#x27; test benchmark evaluates 53 machine learning models, highlighting differences in robustness under common refactoring or input perturbations. The results illustrate how model quality varies across architectures and training regimes, stressing the need for robust evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“Car Wash”测试对53个模型进行了评估，凸显在常见扰动下的鲁棒性差异。结果揭示了不同架构和训练策略的模型在质量上的差异，强调需要更稳健的评估标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opper.ai/images/opper-og.jpg' data-full='https://opper.ai/images/opper-og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47067759' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/azure-sql/langchain-with-sqlvectorstore-example/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft guide to pirating Harry Potter for LLM training (2024) [removed]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软关于为 LLM 训练盗版《哈利·波特》的指南（2024）[已移除]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 358</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47067759'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An apparently removed 2024 developer blog contained an example that could be read as demonstrating how copyrighted text was used for language-model workflows, raising questions about training data licensing. The snippet&#x27;s removal highlights ongoing tensions around permissive examples, copyright law, and responsible documentation for model training practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇已被移除的 2024 年开发博客中包含的示例，可能被解读为展示了如何将受版权保护文本用于语言模型流程，从而引发有关训练数据许可的问题。该内容被删凸显了示例文档、版权法与模型训练责任之间的持续紧张关系。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47040430' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47040430' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SkillsBench: Benchmarking how well agent skills work across diverse tasks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SkillsBench：跨多样任务评测代理技能表现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47040430' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 355</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47040430' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47040430'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SkillsBench introduces a benchmark framework to systematically evaluate AI agent skills across a diverse range of tasks. It aims to better understand how different capabilities generalize beyond specialized scenarios, providing insights into agent robustness and versatility. This work supports advancing more reliable and adaptable AI agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SkillsBench提出了一套评测框架，用于系统性评估AI代理在多样化任务中的技能表现。旨在了解各种能力在专门场景外的泛化效果，为代理的鲁棒性和多功能性提供洞察，助推更可靠适应性强的AI代理发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47154399' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47154399' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.windows.com/windows-insider/2026/01/21/notepad-and-paint-updates-begin-rolling-out-to-windows-insiders/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows 11 Notepad to support Markdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 11 记事本将支持 Markdown</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154399' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 349</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47154399' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 523</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47154399'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Windows Notepad gains native Markdown support, signaling Microsoft&#x27;s push toward developer-friendly tiny tools. The feature makes quick notes with formatting easier for programmers and writers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows 记事本将原生支持 Markdown，体现微软在小型开发工具上的友好取向。此功能让程序员和作者在记笔记时能更方便地处理格式。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dogetrack.info/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DOGE Track</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DOGE Track</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47072967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47072967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 202</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47072967'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lightweight tracker for DOGE-related data—price, charts, and trends—delivered in a clean interface. It highlights how meme assets drive simple, accessible tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个轻量化的 DOGE 跟踪工具，提供价格、图表与趋势等数据，以简洁界面呈现，体现 meme 资产推动的简易可用工具。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47144464' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47144464' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.inceptionlabs.ai/blog/introducing-mercury-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mercury 2: Fast reasoning LLM powered by diffusion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mercury 2：以扩散为驱动的快速推理大模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47144464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 342</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47144464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47144464'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mercury 2 presents fast reasoning for LLMs powered by diffusion techniques, aiming to speed up chain-of-thought reasoning and reduce compute.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mercury 2 通过扩散技术为大型语言模型提供快速推理，旨在提升链式推理速度并降低算力需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/assets/xqKJszO4tzsVEusCSL14t11EJZg.png' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/assets/xqKJszO4tzsVEusCSL14t11EJZg.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47048052' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47048052' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cleantechnica.com/2026/02/15/tesla-sales-down-tremendously-in-uk-norway-netherlands-germany-spain-sweden-denmark-portugal-switzerland/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla Sales Down 55% UK, 58% Spain, 59% Germany, 81% Netherlands, 93% Norway</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉销量大跌：英国降 55%，西班牙 58%，德国 59%，荷兰 81%，挪威 93%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47048052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 337</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47048052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47048052'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reported regional sales figures show steep year-over-year declines for Tesla in multiple European markets, with drops as high as 93% in Norway. These numbers suggest local market dynamics, increased competition, shifting incentives, or production/supply issues are significantly impacting Tesla&#x27;s European sales performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报告显示特斯拉在多个欧洲市场的销量出现大幅同比下降，挪威降幅高达 93%。这些数字表明当地市场动态、竞争加剧、激励变化或产供问题正在显著影响特斯拉在欧洲的销售表现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cleantechnica.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/Tesla-Model-S-Black-Low-Speed-Netherlands-Belgium-Europe-Zach-Shahan-CleanTechnica-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://cleantechnica.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/Tesla-Model-S-Black-Low-Speed-Netherlands-Belgium-Europe-Zach-Shahan-CleanTechnica-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47067031' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47067031' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/8m6AM5qtPMjgTkEeD/my-journey-to-the-microwave-alternate-timeline' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My journey to the microwave alternate timeline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我通往微波炉的平行时间线之旅</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 337</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47067031'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective piece that uses a hypothetical journey to a microwave as a stand-in for exploring alternate timelines and the consequences of small tech decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一篇个人随笔，以微波炉作为隐喻，探讨如何通过日常选择进入不同的时间线。作者反思理性决策与科技进展对人生轨迹的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://res.cloudinary.com/lesswrong-2-0/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1/mirroredImages/8m6AM5qtPMjgTkEeD/xab0mj6lgmlbysspzldj' data-full='https://res.cloudinary.com/lesswrong-2-0/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1/mirroredImages/8m6AM5qtPMjgTkEeD/xab0mj6lgmlbysspzldj' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47038125' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47038125' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tadaima.bearblog.dev/privilege-is-bad-grammar/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Privilege is bad grammar</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特权是一种糟糕的语法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 330</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47038125'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues that &#x27;privilege&#x27; often gets misused in discourse, leading to misunderstandings and unproductive conversations. They suggest reframing privilege in clearer, more precise language to improve communication and foster constructive dialogue. This perspective encourages more thoughtful discussions on social dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为“特权”一词在讨论中常被滥用，引发误解和无效交流。建议用更清晰、精准的语言重新表达“特权”，以提升沟通效果，促进建设性对话。该观点推动更为深思熟虑的社会动态讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/herman-1683556668-0.png' data-full='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/herman-1683556668-0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47139716' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47139716' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonlermen.substack.com/p/large-scale-online-deanonymization' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Large-Scale Online Deanonymization with LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大规模在线去匿名化与大语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47139716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 330</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47139716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47139716'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that large language models, combined with publicly available data, enable dramatic scale in online deanonymization, raising privacy and security concerns. It discusses potential attack surfaces and mitigation strategies, highlighting the tension between AI capabilities and individual anonymity on the open web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出，结合大型语言模型和公开数据，能够在大规模层面开展在线去匿名化，引发隐私与安全担忧。分析了可能的攻击面与缓解策略，强调了AI能力与个人隐私保护之间的矛盾。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47095597' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47095597' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kevinak.se/blog/be-wary-of-bluesky' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Be wary of Bluesky</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>警惕 Bluesky</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47095597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47095597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47095597'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cautionary take on Bluesky, discussing potential pitfalls around governance, data portability, and moderation in a decentralized social platform. It urges readers to consider risks alongside the promised openness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Bluesky 的警惕：警示治理、数据可移植性与去中心化平台上的内容审核等潜在风险，并提醒在看好开放性的同时要关注隐患。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗最高领袖哈梅内伊在以色列袭击中被击毙，结束36年统治</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200879' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 501</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47200879'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports confirm the assassination of Iran&#x27;s top leader, triggering a potential power vacuum and regional instability. The aftermath could reshape Middle East geopolitics and security policy, with implications for sanctions, energy markets, and cyber strategy that tech professionals monitor for risk assessment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，伊朗最高领袖哈梅内伊在以色列发动的袭击中被击毙，可能引发权力真空与区域不稳定。后果可能重塑中东地缘政治、安保政策，并对制裁、能源市场与网络安全策略产生影响，科技从业者需据此评估风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.npr.org/assets/img/2022/09/16/ap17079498857800_wide-9307243360e1a8534d95eb01476568e7f46a2b4a.jpg?s=1400&amp;c=85&amp;f=jpeg' data-full='https://media.npr.org/assets/img/2022/09/16/ap17079498857800_wide-9307243360e1a8534d95eb01476568e7f46a2b4a.jpg?s=1400&amp;c=85&amp;f=jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用旧 Kindle 改造成实时公交到站信息的显示屏</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47199948' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/our-agreement-with-the-department-of-war' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Our Agreement with the Department of War</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我們與战争部的協議</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47199948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 324</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47199948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47199948'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI outlines its relationship with government defense entities, framing responsible AI use, safety, and oversight in sensitive applications. The document clarifies guardrails and collaboration boundaries, illustrating how defense partnerships shape AI deployment in high-stakes contexts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 概述了与政府防务机构的关系，强调在敏感应用中对 AI 的负责任使用、安全与监督。文档澄清了防护边界与协作界线，显示了防务伙伴关系如何影响高风险场景下的 AI 部署。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47186031' data-tags='Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47186031' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/02/27/nx-s1-5729118/trump-anthropic-pentagon-openai-ai-weapons-ban' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>President Trump bans Anthropic from use in government systems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普总统禁止 Anthropic 在政府系统中的使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47186031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47186031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47186031'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>President Trump bans Anthropic from use in government systems, signaling a policy move that could affect procurement and access to AI tools for public sector. It illustrates how political shifts shape AI vendor relationships.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特朗普总统宣布禁止 Anthropic 在政府系统中使用，可能影响公共部门的采购与对 AI 工具的访问。这一定性转变也凸显政治因素对 AI 供应商关系的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/2346x1320+0+93/resize/1400/quality/85/format/jpeg/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fb4%2Fea%2Fbfec70974d408a48598215de7c04%2Fap26036779392489.jpg' data-full='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/2346x1320+0+93/resize/1400/quality/85/format/jpeg/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fb4%2Fea%2Fbfec70974d408a48598215de7c04%2Fap26036779392489.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47194611' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47194611' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nanoclaw.dev/blog/nanoclaw-security-model' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t trust AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别信任 AI 代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47194611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47194611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47194611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of the reliability and safety risks of autonomous AI agents, showing why you shouldn&#x27;t over-rely on them for critical tasks. The piece advocates robust architectures, verification, and tamper-resistance to prevent misbehavior or exploitation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨自治 AI 代理的可靠性与安全风险，解释为何不应过度依赖它们完成关键任务。文章主张构建更强健的架构、可验证性与防篡改设计，以防止误操作或被利用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.guidelabs.ai/post/steerling-8b-base-model-release/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Steerling-8B, a language model that can explain any token it generates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Steerling-8B，一种能解释其生成的每个标记的语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47131225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47131225' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47131225'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Steerling-8B offers a built-in explainability layer that can justify each token it generates, addressing a key pain point in LLM trust and debugging. The project explores how model transparency can inform better prompting, debugging, and safety checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Steerling-8B 提供内置的可解释性层，能够为其生成的每个标记给出解释，有助于提升 LLM 的可信度、调试与安全性。该项目探索透明度在提示设计和错误排查中的应用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/pgdogdev/pgdog' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: PgDog – Scale Postgres without changing the app</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47123631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47123631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47123631'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PgDog offers a method to scale PostgreSQL capacity without requiring changes to client apps, potentially via a sharding/proxy approach. If effective, it could simplify scaling for existing deployments and reduce migration risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PgDog 提供一种在不修改客户端应用的前提下扩大 PostgreSQL 容量的方法，可能通过代理层或分片实现。若有效，将简化现有部署的扩容工作，降低迁移风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsencrypt.org/2026/02/18/dns-persist-01.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DNS-Persist-01: A New Model for DNS-Based Challenge Validation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DNS-Persist-01：一种新的基于 DNS 的挑战验证模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47064047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 315</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47064047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47064047'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt introduced DNS-Persist-01, a new ACME challenge type that allows DNS-based validation to persist beyond short-lived records, simplifying automation for some use cases. This change can reduce flakiness in DNS validation workflows and help environments where DNS propagation or record stability is a problem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt 推出 DNS-Persist-01，一种新的 ACME 挑战类型，使基于 DNS 的验证能在短期记录之外保持，从而简化某些自动化场景。此改动可减少 DNS 验证流程中的不稳定性，利于 DNS 传播或记录稳定性较差的环境。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gamedate.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gamedate – A site to revive dead multiplayer games</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47096167'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gamedate provides a venue for reviving abandoned multiplayer games by coordinating communities, matchmaking, and mods around legacy titles. It highlights community-driven preservation as a way to extend the life of classic games and reduce waste.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gamedate 为复兴已停运的多人游戏提供平台，帮助社区组织、重建匹配与模组，让经典作品重新活跃。此举体现通过社区力量延长游戏生命、降低资源浪费的趋势。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47074389'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparative analysis argues that savings alone can&#x27;t shield economies from shocks; policy mix, diversification, and resilience investments matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对比分析认为，仅靠储蓄无法完全抵御经济冲击；需要多元化政策、韧性投资与结构性改革。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/paypal-discloses-data-breach-exposing-users-personal-information/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PayPal discloses data breach that exposed user info for 6 months</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PayPal 披露数据泄露：用户信息暴露长达 6 个月</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47087719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47087719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47087719'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PayPal disclosed a data breach exposing user information for six months, prompting guidance for affected users to monitor accounts and enable alerts. The incident underscores fintech security challenges and the importance of timely incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PayPal 公开披露数据泄露，用户信息暴露长达六个月，敦促受影响用户监控账户并开启通知。事件凸显金融科技领域的安全挑战及快速响应的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47146487' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.janestreet.com/can-you-reverse-engineer-our-neural-network/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can you reverse engineer our neural network?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你能反向工程我们的神经网络吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47146487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47146487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 193</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47146487'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece examines how much you can infer about a trained neural network from its outputs, including architecture, weights, and training data. It highlights practical limits and security implications for protecting IP and guarding against model extraction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨仅凭输出能否推断出一个训练完成的神经网络的架构、权重和训练数据，以及这对知识产权保护和模型提取安全的现实限制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.janestreet.com/can-you-reverse-engineer-our-neural-network/puzzle.png' data-full='https://blog.janestreet.com/can-you-reverse-engineer-our-neural-network/puzzle.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47038588' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notnotp.com/notes/use-protocols-not-services/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Use protocols, not services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用协议，而非服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47038588'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay advocates for designing systems around open, standardized protocols instead of relying on proprietary services. Emphasizing protocols encourages interoperability, decentralization, and user control, aligning with foundational internet principles. This argument supports building resilient, user-empowering technology ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张围绕开放标准协议设计系统，而非依赖专有服务。强调协议促进互操作性、去中心化和用户控制，契合互联网基础理念。该观点支持构建更具弹性和用户自主权的技术生态。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47092203' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Business'>

<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://juno-labs.com/blogs/every-company-building-your-ai-assistant-is-an-ad-company' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every company building your AI assistant is now an ad company</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47092203'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that AI assistant developers monetize primarily through data-driven advertising and targeted services, raising privacy concerns. Calls for greater transparency, user controls, and consented data usage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出开发 AI 助手的公司多通过数据驱动的广告及定向服务来变现，带来隐私风险。呼吁提高透明度、提供用户控制与明确的同意数据使用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/LadybirdBrowser/ladybird/issues/933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Closing this as we are no longer pursuing Swift adoption</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关闭此项：我们不再推进 Swift 采纳</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 295</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47067678'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project issue announcing the decision to stop pursuing Swift adoption cites practical reasons—likely maintenance burden, ecosystem fit, or contributor availability—leading to reverting course. It highlights the trade-offs teams face when evaluating language migrations and the importance of aligning tooling choices with project capacity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一条议题声明决定不再推进 Swift 的采纳，理由可能是维护负担、生态适配或贡献者可用性等实际因素，因而选择回退。此事凸显团队在评估语言迁移时的权衡，以及将工具选择与项目能力对齐的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/bd7753566bc9dda581e2395c7ad9fe1b8dd77c835a32a361e79ca73d3871572e/LadybirdBrowser/ladybird/issues/933' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/bd7753566bc9dda581e2395c7ad9fe1b8dd77c835a32a361e79ca73d3871572e/LadybirdBrowser/ladybird/issues/933' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47129727' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47129727' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://writings.stephenwolfram.com/2026/02/making-wolfram-tech-available-as-a-foundation-tool-for-llm-systems/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Making Wolfram tech available as a foundation tool for LLM systems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 Wolfram 技术成为 LLM 系统的基础工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47129727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47129727' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47129727'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wolfram&#x27;s tech is being embedded as a foundational tool for LLM systems, enabling precise computation, data lookup, and symbolic reasoning within AI assistants. This approach enhances accuracy and explainability while shaping how future copilots reason about mathematics, science, and data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wolfram 的计算与知识图谱能力正被整合进 LLM 系统，作为基础工具以提供精确计算、数据检索与符号推理。此举提升了答案的准确性与可解释性，并将塑造未来助手在数学、科学与数据领域的推理方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://content.wolfram.com/sites/43/2026/02/FoundationTool-seo-v2a.png' data-full='https://content.wolfram.com/sites/43/2026/02/FoundationTool-seo-v2a.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The larger deal was abandoned in favor of a smaller investment, reshaping partnerships and governance in the AI ecosystem. The move signals recalibrated expectations for collaboration and access to compute power.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>原本的高额交易被放弃，改为较小规模的投资，重新塑造了 AI 生态中的伙伴关系与治理结构，折射出对合作与算力获取的再评估。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c5yj2kzkrj0o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iran students stage first large anti-government protests since deadly crackdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗学生在致命镇压后首次举行大规模反政府抗议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 292</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 454</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47111067'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iranian students staged what appears to be the largest anti-government protests since a deadly crackdown, signaling continued unrest and potential for online mobilization. The demonstrations spotlight how students remain a catalyst for political change, despite restrictions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊朗学生举行自致命镇压以来规模最大的反政府抗议，显示持续的社会动荡与线上动员的潜力。示威凸显学生群体仍是推动政治变革的重要推动力，尽管面临限制。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/lifeandstyle/2026/feb/22/a-new-start-after-60-i-baked-a-pie-every-day-for-a-year-and-it-changed-my-life' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I baked a pie every day for a year</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我每天烤一个馅饼一年，改变了我的生活</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47128645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 291</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47128645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47128645'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Baking a pie every day for a year became a catalyst for routine, creativity, and personal growth, revealing how small, repeatable practices can unlock new habits. The experiment offers transferable lessons on discipline, iteration, and finding joy in craft.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>每天烤一个馅饼持续一年成为培养日常规律、创造力与个人成长的催化剂，展示了以小而重复的练习可以养成新习惯。此实验提供可迁移的自律、迭代与在制作中发现乐趣的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/13b25becf38eae040d804aba8ec782ba8cd66988/654_0_3335_2668/master/3335.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=3489c87db87da769fb108a825e59ff73' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/13b25becf38eae040d804aba8ec782ba8cd66988/654_0_3335_2668/master/3335.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=3489c87db87da769fb108a825e59ff73' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47200904' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dl.acm.org/doi/fullHtml/10.1145/238386.238611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Windows 95 user interface: A case study in usability engineering (1996)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 95 用户界面的可用性工程案例研究（1996）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47200904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47200904'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how Windows 95 UI decisions were tested and validated, illustrating early usability engineering practices and their impact on modern design. The article distills lessons on consistency, feedback, and discoverability that still influence UX today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 Windows 95 的用户界面设计与可用性工程方法，揭示早期 UX 实践及其对现代设计的影响。文中提炼出一致性、反馈与易发现性的经验，至今仍影响用户体验。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47130860' data-tags='Hardware,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47130860' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dork.dev/posts/2026-02-20-ported-coreboot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I Ported Coreboot to the ThinkPad X270</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我已将 Coreboot 移植到 ThinkPad X270</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47130860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47130860' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47130860'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal project ports Coreboot to the ThinkPad X270, replacing the official BIOS with open-source firmware. It demonstrates the feasibility and challenges of reviving older hardware with modern, auditable firmware and can improve boot speed and security, though feature support may lag.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>个人项目将 Coreboot 移植到 ThinkPad X270，用开源固件取代原厂 BIOS。此举展示了在较旧硬件上实现可验证、快速启动与更高安全性的可行性，同时也存在驱动、硬件兼容性等挑战。</p>
