<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA announces overhaul of Artemis program amid safety concerns and delays, signaling a shift in lunar exploration strategy and risk management. Expect revised timelines and funding allocations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NASA 在阿尔忒弥斯计划面临安全顾虑与延迟之际宣布改革，预示着月球探测策略和风险管理的调整，预计时间表与资金分配将随之变化。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://collapseofindustrialcivilization.com/2026/02/16/americas-oligarchic-techno-feudal-elite-are-attempting-to-build-a-twenty-first-century-fascist-state/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Techno‑feudal elite are attempting to build a twenty‑first‑century fascist state</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技贵族试图建造二十一世纪的法西斯国家</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47198912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 286</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47198912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47198912'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that a tech-elite class seeks to consolidate power through surveillance and platform control, linking economic disparity with political risk. It calls for vigilance about governance, antitrust, and civic safeguards in the age of digital power.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张科技精英阶层正通过监控与平台控制来巩固权力，将经济不平等与政治风险联系在一起。呼吁在数字时代加强治理、反垄断与公民防护。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ahmedeldin.substack.com/p/the-israeli-spyware-firm-that-accidentally' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Israeli spyware firm that accidentally just exposed itself</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以色列间谍软件公司意外暴露自身</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47033976' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 286</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47033976' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 325</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47033976'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Israeli spyware firm unintentionally revealed sensitive information about its operations, potentially compromising their surveillance tactics. Such leaks highlight the risks and ethical concerns surrounding commercial spyware vendors in the cyber intelligence ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家以色列间谍软件公司意外泄露了有关其运营的敏感信息，可能暴露其监控手段。这类泄露凸显了商业间谍软件厂商在网络情报领域面临的风险和伦理问题。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47184480' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.beyondthejoke.co.uk/content/17193/red-dwarf-rob-grant' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rob Grant, creator of Red Dwarf, has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《红矮星》创作者罗布·格兰特去世</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47184480'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rob Grant, Red Dwarf creator, has died; tributes pour in for the sci-fi writer who helped shape British comedic sci-fi.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Red Dwarf》创作者罗布·格兰特去世，众多读者与科幻爱好者缅怀这位塑造英国科幻喜剧的作家。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47130208' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47130208' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/ai-added-basically-zero-to-us-economic-growth-last-year-goldman-sachs-says-2000725380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Added &#x27;Basically Zero&#x27; to US Economic Growth Last Year, Goldman Sachs Says</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高盛称 AI 对去年美国经济增长几乎没有贡献</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47130208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47130208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47130208'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Goldman Sachs argues that AI contributed essentially zero to U.S. economic growth last year, despite hype around AI-driven productivity. The data suggests that the macro wind is still dominated by fundamentals like labor, capital, and investment cycles, with AI&#x27;s productivity gains taking time to materialize.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高盛表示，尽管 AI 热潮高涨，但去年美国经济增长几乎没有来自 AI 的贡献，AI 的生产率提升仍在滞后期。结论强调经济增长仍受劳动、资本和投资周期等基本因素驱动，AI 的收益需要时间显现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gizmodo.com/app/uploads/2024/08/samaltman.png' data-full='https://gizmodo.com/app/uploads/2024/08/samaltman.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47191414' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/live/2026/02/28/world/iran-strikes-trump' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. and Israel Conduct Strikes on Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美以对伊朗发动打击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47191414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 282</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47191414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47191414'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. and Israel conducted strikes on Iran, highlighting a tense escalation in the region with potential implications for global security and AI/tech supply chains depending on retaliation and geopolitical alignment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美以对伊朗发动打击，显示该地区局势升级及对全球安全与科技供应链可能产生的连带影响，具体取决于后续行动。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47175931' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-47175931' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/02/26/technology/google-deepmind-letter-pentagon.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google workers seek &#x27;red lines&#x27; on military A.I., echoing Anthropic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌员工呼吁在军事 AI 上设立红线，与 Anthropic 呼应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47175931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 282</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47175931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-47175931'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google staff push for red lines on military AI, echoing safety commitments seen in Anthropic. The debate centers on dual-use technology, governance, and potential impacts on Google&#x27;s AI strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌员工呼吁在军事 AI 上设立红线，与 Anthropic 的安全承诺相呼应。讨论聚焦双用途技术、治理以及对谷歌 AI 策略的潜在影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
